A power-path key switch may serve one selected electrical isolation function, but not automatically all hydraulic/gravity/thermal/drive energy. A key controlling only PLC enable remains a control-circuit device regardless of catalog family. Keep `SRS-ACCESS` key sequence and `SRS-RESTART` independent of `SRS-ENERGY` isolation. Selected-machine `IN-01..10 UNKNOWN`; all physical `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, release HOLD.


## 11AF. Trapped-key release timer versus stopped-motion sensor (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED, Rockwell 440T:** the electronic timed-delay unit releases a key after a configured elapsed duration, whereas the stopped-motion unit uses inductive sensors and CU2 monitoring; historical CU2 instructions describe stopped-motion detection **followed by** a selected delay. Exact family and product cut sheets differ on maximum delay; no generic number is accepted. See [ETU/SMU/CU2 source audit](../research/4400_TRAPPED_KEY_TIMED_RELEASE_VS_SENSED_STOP_MOTION_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS-RELEASE / DEP / PHY / VAL:** require exact stop event, sensor target and coupling, timer/threshold/configuration, fault behavior, shared supplies, selected key solenoid, independent `PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS` witnesses and gravity/stored-energy residuals. `TIME ELAPSED != MACHINE STOPPED`; `MONITORED SHAFT STOPPED != ALL HAZARDOUS LOADS RESTRAINED`; `KEY FREE LED != PHYSICAL SAFE STATE`. Power-loss egress, deliberate rearm and maintenance LOTO remain separate. No selected-machine stopping criterion, test, physical release or PL/SIL is claimed.


## 11AG. Stopped-motion sensor mechanical coverage, plausibility cut-off and key-release authority (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED, version/product scope:** Rockwell CU2-IN001 (historical Issue 1) shows two proximity sensors, separate brackets and stopped-motion detection followed by a configured timer; it does **not** publish an exhaustive missing-pulse/common-supply/coupling-failure release truth table. Rockwell 440T-IN035A-EN-P (April 2018) requires installation-specific risk assessment and periodic inspection. Pilz PNOZ s30 1001715-EN-24 (pp. 11–12, 73–74, 92–99, 121–122) explicitly calls out encoder slip/shear-pin hazards, configurable low-frequency plausibility-check cut-off, stuck-track/frequency-ratio diagnostics and sensor-subsystem CCF analysis. Schmersal FWS 1206 catalog/ISD information describes product-specific wire-break/frequency-disagreement diagnostics and a supplementary standstill signal; its archived product status and incomplete direct operating-manual access prevent universal transfer. [Source and failure-path audit](../research/4400_STOP_MOTION_SENSOR_COVERAGE_FAULT_AND_COUPLING_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md); [technician diagnostic field card](4400_STOP_MOTION_FALSE_STANDSTILL_TECHNICIAN_DIAGNOSTIC_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**Required traceability:** create or update `SRS-RELEASE` with the actual initiating stop event, selected device/firmware/manual, pulse/threshold/timer and explicit fault states; `DEP/CCF` for sensor power, mounting, target, motor–coupling–hazard path, shaft slip and low-speed diagnostic blind spots; `AUTH` for independent release ownership and final elements; `HF` for operator wait time and foreseeable defeat; `PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS/ESCAPE/ENERGY/RESTART` for separately witnessed physical propositions; `VAL/PT` for actual fault- and mode-specific proof. A stopped motor shaft, closed monitor output or green key-free indicator does not establish downstream spindle, ram, gravity load, guard egress or maintenance isolation. Product defaults (including Pilz basic-configuration automatic output start) are **not** permission for a machine to restart without deliberate independent rearm and a fresh production demand.

**Frozen boundary:** do not infer exact CU2 sensor-loss response from “fail-safe and cross monitored”; do not infer Pilz low-speed plausibility from higher-speed diagnostics; do not treat two sensors sharing one failed mechanical member as independent load witnesses; do not promote an ordinary LinuxCNC/FPGA speed reading into safety release authority. No invented selected thresholds, stopping distances, PL/SIL, diagnostic coverage or hydraulic truth tables.

**Gate unchanged:** `IN-01..10 UNKNOWN`, all `PHY` unproven, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, `G0/G1 HOLD`, `G2 NOT RUN`, `G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED`, `G5 HOLD`. No personnel-exposed operation or physical fault insertion authorized.


## 11AH. Monitor output auto-reenable versus independent fresh machine start (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED, Pilz PNOZ s30 1001715-EN-24 §§5.3.9/7.7/8.5.4/8.7.4:** automatic output start can reclose a monitor output after a condition becomes permissive; the manufacturer explicitly requires external circuit measures to prevent unexpected restart when a safeguard resets. Monitored-edge S34 start is an **output** mode, not a substitute for machine-level occupancy, stop/hold, safety rearm or fresh production start. The two basic configurations use automatic output start; older-device parameter transfers can change safety behavior. [Original exact manual audit](../research/4400_PNOZ_S30_AUTOMATIC_OUTPUT_REENABLE_VS_FRESH_MACHINE_RESTART_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS/AUTH/DEP/VAL gate:** freeze selected output assignments, each start mode, S34 and feedback wiring, version/CRC and actual downstream final elements. For E-stop release, guard close, power recovery, sensor fault clear, maintenance return, and a held pedal/start bit, independently show that a permissive monitor output **cannot by itself cause hazardous motion** and that ordinary LinuxCNC commands from a previous episode are invalidated. Preserve physical `PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS/ESCAPE/ENERGY` separately from `PHY-RESTART`. Record operator reset ergonomics and nuisance-trip incentives under `HF`. No selected-machine test or automatic-release claim; `IN-01..10 UNKNOWN`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, `G0/G1 HOLD`, `G2 NOT RUN`, `G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED`, `G5 HOLD`.

## 11AI. Brake feedback fault, conditional torque retention and hydraulic lowering scope (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED:** Rockwell Studio 5000 Logix Designer v38 SBC help defines two active-low brake outputs, TOR, two brake feedback channels, and a negative STO-to-SBC delay for vertical loads that exceeds selected brake engagement time. A brake-feedback fault forces brake outputs OFF while **TOR retains its prior state**; in the documented fault-before-torque-off example TOR is not asserted, permitting the motion task to continue holding a gravity load. This is a conditional selected-product behavior, **not** a rule that torque always persists on fault or loss of power. Siemens SINAMICS S120 12/2018 FW5.2 §4.2.12 SBT deliberately applies test torque and checks encoder motion; its manual expressly excludes SBT on SINAMICS HLA. Parker HAS HY08-4005-M11 pp. 35–36 distinguishes a PO check for load holding from controlled load lowering and warns of ratcheting in the HAS application. See [cross-architecture source audit](../research/4400_BRAKE_FAULT_TORQUE_RETENTION_AND_HYDRAULIC_LOAD_CONTROL_SCOPE_2026-10-23.md) and [technician card](4400_BRAKE_FAULT_GRAVITY_HOLD_AND_LOAD_LOWERING_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS/AUTH/DEP/PHY/VAL requirement:** For any selected gravity-loaded axis, map motor/valve command, independently qualified safety controller, feedback, torque/pressure source, brake/valve/physical restraint, mechanical coupling, gravity, power-loss and simultaneous fault paths. Preserve separate propositions for stop, hold, access, energy isolation, and fresh restart. A safe brake *test* is an energized, potentially moving procedure, not an automatic permission for personnel exposure. A hydraulic check/monitor cannot be assigned a controlled-lowering safety function by analogy. Selected-device times/thresholds, exact circuit, brake torque, PL/SIL, diagnostic coverage and machine-specific test response remain **UNKNOWN**. All selected-machine IN-01..10 unknown, VAL/PT NOT RUN, G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, G5 HOLD.

## 11AJ. Bypassable hydraulic load-hold device and orientation-dependent energy zones (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED, Parker HAS HY08-4005-M11 pp. 35–37:** its circuit code N (“no load holding”) uses an R-coded pilot-operated check with **manual override engaged**, allowing bidirectional flow. A tamper-resistant cover is available if the override is an application problem. The manifold's a/b cavities map to push/pull chambers according to actuator orientation A–D. The same manual explicitly distinguishes static PO-check holding from controlled lowering. [Exact source audit](../research/4400_PARKER_HAS_LOAD_HOLD_OVERRIDE_CODE_AND_MANIFOLD_ORIENTATION_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS/DEP/HF/PHY/VAL:** Before crediting a visible check valve, capture exact installed circuit/order code, manual override position/defeat resistance, manifold orientation, port-to-chamber topology, pilot supply and hose paths. Record a service-mode return-to-normal check and independent physical load restraint. A tamper-resistant cover is not a certified safety function, a cartridge's presence is not proof it is active, and a gauge on the wrong port is not proof of energy removal. This is a HAS manufacturer example, not a universal hydraulic truth table. Selected machine IN-01..10 UNKNOWN, VAL/PT NOT RUN, G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, G5 HOLD.

## 11AK. Energized brake testing during servicing versus maintenance isolation (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED:** Siemens S120 Safety Integrated FW5.2 12/2018 §4.2.12 requires drive pulses, controlled brake opening/closing and torque application for its SBT. Official OSHA 29 CFR 1910.147(f)(1), when applicable to a covered servicing/maintenance task requiring temporary reenergization, requires clearing tools, removing people, authorized lock removal, bounded testing and return to deenergized/locked energy control before servicing resumes. [Source and application distinction](../research/4400_BRAKE_FAULT_TORQUE_RETENTION_AND_HYDRAULIC_LOAD_CONTROL_SCOPE_2026-10-23.md) and [open-book worker challenge](4400_BRAKE_FAULT_GRAVITY_HOLD_AND_LOAD_LOWERING_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS/HF/VAL:** Distinguish normal production SBT from temporary maintenance test energization. An HMI “test” command does not waive per-worker lockout, personnel clearance, physical gravity restraint or independent restart. Selected site procedure and actual test safety arrangement remain UNKNOWN; no energized machine test authorized.

## 11AL. Counterbalance pilot/thermal relief, cylinder versus hydraulic motor and independent load witness (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED (product-scoped):** Sun Hydraulics June 2023 technical tip separates non-modulating pilot-to-open check cartridges from modulating counterbalance cartridges, documents load-port thermal/overpressure relief and selected PO-check pilot-piston leakage variants. Parker HY15-3502/US LM2–LM5 describes load-dependent pilot opening, thermal relief and **hydraulic motor internal leakage requiring a separate mechanical brake** in its illustrated motor application. Parker HAS HY08-4005-M11 explicitly warns that load-hold cartridges can retain cylinder pressure and must not be removed until all internal pressures are relieved. See [source and failure-path audit](../research/4400_COUNTERBALANCE_PILOT_THERMAL_RELIEF_AND_MOTOR_BRAKE_BOUNDARY_2026-10-23.md) and [open-book technician field card](4400_PILOT_THERMAL_RELIEF_AND_MOTOR_BRAKE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**ENE/DEP/ARC/SRS:** `pump OFF` is not a measured `pilot P3=0`; `pilot P3=0` is not an indefinite physical lock; `counterbalance installed` is not a universal brake or maintenance isolator. Map exact P1 load/P2 downstream/P3 pilot, trapped and cross-connected pressure, thermal relief, valve variant/override, actuator leakage, hose/valve placement, brake and independent physical load path. A thermal relief function must not be defeated merely to suppress motion/pressure symptoms. A healthy controller valve tag is not a load witness.

**PHY-HOLD/PHY-ENERGY/VAL/HF/CHG:** Require independent physical load restraint and service-zone pressure evidence, with test points and override state clearly marked and easily restored. Any selected valve, pilot route, motor, brake, hose or port change requires requalification. All selected-machine IN-01..10 UNKNOWN, PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS/ESCAPE/ENERGY/RESTART UNKNOWN, VAL/PT NOT RUN, G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, G5 HOLD. No personnel-exposed operation authorized.

## 11AM. Motor counterbalance opening before mechanical brake release (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED, Parker HY15-3502/US LM5 illustrative example:** a load-dependent 10:1 counterbalance can begin opening at a lower pilot pressure than the mechanical brake's release pressure, so a motor can try to rotate against an applied brake. Parker's illustrated equal-area 1:1 motor-brake alternative avoids that *particular* sequencing problem but explicitly **lacks built-in thermal relief**. The two valve families cannot be swapped based on “counterbalance present” alone. See [follow-on primary-source correction](../research/4400_COUNTERBALANCE_PILOT_THERMAL_RELIEF_AND_MOTOR_BRAKE_BOUNDARY_2026-10-23.md) and [technician scenario 8](4400_PILOT_THERMAL_RELIEF_AND_MOTOR_BRAKE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS/DEP/PHY/VAL:** Freeze actual brake-release/valve-opening order, selected pilot routes, pressure/spike/thermal protections and independent physical brake/load witnesses before assigning a machine-level safety claim. Catalog illustration pressures are **not** installed-machine setpoints. No machine tests, pressure settings, safety ratings or personnel access authorization follow from this paper.

## 11AN. TDP/TEP fail-safe adjective, pilot supply and D3DW manual attribution (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED, Parker:** Official TDP product page simultaneously advertises a power-down “fail safe position” and says pilot pressure is needed to ensure closure. Parker 2024 MSG11-5715-697/UK identifies TDP main proportional valve versus TEP with separate monitored shut-off, X pilot supply/Y drain, and a reliable electronics-supply switching requirement for safety-related control. Later printed pages in the same combined manual describe **D3DW**, not generic TDP, when stating spring-centered power-loss return. See [product/paragraph provenance audit](../research/4400_PARKER_TDP_TEP_FAILSAFE_PILOT_AND_D3DW_MANUAL_SCOPE_TRAP_2026-10-23.md) and [technician scenario 9](4400_PILOT_THERMAL_RELIEF_AND_MOTOR_BRAKE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**SRS/DEP/ARC/PHY:** Require exact TDP/TEP/D3DW order code and valve role, selected power/pilot/drain/return behavior, independent safety demand/final element, physical stopping/holding and pressure-zone witnesses. `MANUAL TEXT FOUND != CORRECT COMPONENT ATTRIBUTION`; `FAIL SAFE VALVE POSITION != PHYSICAL MACHINE SAFE STATE`. No transfer of a D3DW paragraph into a TDP machine safety claim. Selected-machine IN/PHY UNKNOWN; VAL/PT NOT RUN.


## 11AO. Press-brake guarding applicability, historical safe-distance exceptions and maintenance scope (2026-10-23)

**DOC-CONFIRMED (OSHA, date- and jurisdiction-scoped):** 29 CFR 1910.217(a)(5) explicitly excludes press brakes and hydraulic/pneumatic power presses from **that mechanical-power-press section**, while OSHA's press-brake interpretations identify 1910.212 safeguarding obligations. OSHA's 1986 letter states a historical six-inch safe-distance floor but was edited to point to the **1997 CPL 02-01-025** directive; the latter's four-inch alternative is conditional on documented physical-device infeasibility, limited custom production and supporting work procedures, training, supervision and inspections. Neither figure is a universal sensor positioning or machine stopping-distance criterion. OSHA 1910.147(d)(5)–(6) independently addresses stored/reaccumulating energy and verification during covered maintenance. See [source applicability audit](../research/4400_PRESS_BRAKE_OSHA_SCOPE_SAFE_DISTANCE_AND_MAINTENANCE_TRANSFER_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md) and [open-book field exercise](4400_PRESS_BRAKE_GUARDING_SCOPE_AND_SERVICE_ZONE_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-23.md).

**SCOPE/HAZ/SRS/PHY/ENE/HF/VAL/CHG:** Freeze selected machine, task, jurisdiction, standard edition and regulatory applicability before importing any numeric criterion. Guard front/rear/sheet sweep/helper hazards by actual geometry and mode; treat safety reset, ordinary LinuxCNC/FPGA control, load holding, physical access, maintenance isolation and fresh start as different propositions. Require pressure-zone and independent mechanical load witness before servicing, continued verification where energy can reaccumulate, and safe operator/maintainer workflow instead of foreseeable guard bypass. All IN-01..10 and PHY selected-machine propositions remain UNKNOWN; VAL/PT NOT RUN; G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, G5 HOLD. No attended hazardous operation authorized.


### 11AP — OEM ram support, LOTO, entrapment rescue and manual-reference cross-check (2026-10-23)

- **Documented selected-OEM example, not a reusable machine setting:** Cincinnati EM-560 (N-06-14) printed 9-1 separates physically supported ram, electrical isolation and hydraulic residual-energy checks. Its 10%-of-machine-capacity support statement and model-specific test-port/bleed timing must **not** be transferred to a different machine or confused with ram weight. See [OEM source audit](../research/4400_CINCINNATI_PROFORM_RAM_RESTRAINT_LOTO_RESCUE_MANUAL_ERRATA_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md).
- **Independent rescue proposition:** the same manual separates entrapment with/without power, with special mechanical support and hazardous-fluid caveats. Rescue is neither an E-stop reset nor ordinary maintenance LOTO; qualified machine-specific planning is required.
- **Manufacturer editorial cross-check:** printed maintenance text misidentifies multiple Figure 1-1/1-2/1-3 item numbers against the manual's own legends. Quarantine ambiguous locators and require actual machine/OEM verification before use. Do not silently correct a safety instruction by guesswork.
- **Contrasting manufacturer scope:** Haas HPB 2026 public safety and maintenance pages mention lockout, hydraulic relief and POWER OFF, but do not furnish a universal ram support or pressure-zone map. “POWER OFF” is not `PHY-ENERGY` evidence.
- **Human factors:** provide approved support storage/handling, clear labels, low-friction guard reinstatement and usable service access so bypassing protection is not the easiest workflow.
- **Release:** `IN-01..10 UNKNOWN`, `PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS/ESCAPE/ENERGY/RESTART UNKNOWN`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, `G0/G1 HOLD`, `G2 NOT RUN`, `G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED`, `G5 HOLD`. No selected physical tests, acceptance thresholds or machine authorization. [Open-book technician exercise](4400_RAM_SUPPORT_LOTO_AND_POWER_LOSS_RESCUE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-23.md).


### 11AQ — OEM optional mute, programmable stations, ADI plug and hydraulic add-on boundaries (2026-10-23)

The [PROFORM+ option-source audit](../research/4400_PROFORM_OPTIONAL_MUTING_ADI_PLUG_FOOTSWITCH_AND_HYDRAULIC_ADDON_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md) establishes from official 2014 OEM text that presence sensing and rear guarding may be optional, guard mute is position-dependent and explicitly removes point-of-operation protection, footswitch selection can change by production step, a removable ADI default plug exists with **undocumented safety semantics**, and hydraulic Power Clamp/Auto Crown add energy paths. None transfers as a selected OpenPressBrake safety circuit. Require `OPT-01..08` installed-option inventory, mode/step-specific `SRS/PHY`, independent alternative protection during muting, plug pinout/change authority, and auxiliary-energy isolation/holding witnesses. **No live plug substitution or invented wiring.** Red GUARD OFF is a diagnostic indication, not permission for hazardous access. Selected `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, external evaluation OPEN, G0/G1 HOLD.


### 11AR — Independent TRUMPF B35 guard-state asymmetry, mute, remote UI and disassembly physical witness (2026-10-23)

[2023 OEM source audit](../research/4400_TRUMPF_B35_SIDE_DOOR_ASYMMETRY_MUTE_REMOTE_SUPPORT_AND_DISASSEMBLY_AUDIT_2026-10-23.md) and [open-book field card](4400_TRUMPF_B35_ACCESS_REMOTE_DIAGNOSTIC_AND_DISASSEMBLY_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-23.md) show: one side door versus both side doors versus rear door have **different described reactions**; BendGuard becomes inactive below mute with reduced beam speed; backgauge drives can remain supplied while STO is active; remote service can access UI but the manual does not establish remote motion authority; disassembly can release an unbraked assembly or drop the press beam after hydraulic components are removed. Never infer that a single `guard_ok` or `estop` bit proves all physical propositions. Require guard/mode-specific SRS, remote-session authority boundaries, selected physical beam support, energy isolation and independent fresh restart. All selected `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, external evaluation OPEN, no personnel-exposed operation authorized.

### 11AT — LinuxCNC 2.9.10 remote command, stale status and reset authority (2026-10-23)

[Exact pinned-source trace](../research/4400_LINUXCNCRSH_2910_REMOTE_COMMAND_AUTHORITY_AND_STALE_STATUS_SOURCE_TRACE_2026-10-23.md) and [eight-case field card](4400_LINUXCNCRSH_REMOTE_COMMAND_STALE_STATE_AND_REARM_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-23.md): `src/emc/usr_intf/emcrsh.cc` tag `v2.9.10`, blob `81b387c20f297d43b7fb63ad26fcf4d25ec0aa62`, shows `INADDR_ANY` TCP listener, documented default cleartext connection/enable passwords, software socket-authority gate, SET handlers for `ESTOP/MACHINE/JOG/MDI/RUN/OVERRIDE_LIMITS`, optional `EMC_UPDATE_NONE` stale GET state, and disconnect without an independent physical safety action in the inspected path. **SOURCE-CONFIRMED** is the actual call/branch structure, **DOC-CONFIRMED** the 2.9 man page; any descriptor-lifetime anomaly is **INFERENCE/UNTESTED**, not an asserted exploit. Require explicit network capability inventory and backend read-only allowlist, source timestamp/quality and local deliberate motion authority; independent safety reset, final elements, ram restraint and maintenance isolation remain separate. `NET-01..08 NOT RUN`, `IN/OPT/PHY UNKNOWN`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, external 2520–25F0 evaluation OPEN, no exposed hazardous operation authorized.


**11AT pinned Task/NML extension:** `shcom.cc` v2.9.10 `sendEstopReset()` sends `EMC_TASK_SET_STATE(ESTOP_RESET)`; `emctaskmain.cc` dispatches it; `emctask.cc` `emcTaskSetState()` calls `emcAuxEstopOff()` and normal Task/IO cleanup. `determineState()` reads auxiliary E-stop and trajectory enable, not a complete physical safe-state witness. Exact pinned Git blob SHAs and line ranges in the source audit. No actual selected safety relay/valve/guard response established.


### 11AU — ISO 13855:2024 separation distance does not cover gravity, mechanical failure or emissions (2026-10-24)

[Edition/scope and manufacturer stop-time source reconciliation](../research/4400_ISO13855_2024_SEPARATION_DISTANCE_SCOPE_STOP_TIME_AND_GRAVITY_EXCEPTION_2026-10-24.md) and [technician card](4400_STOP_TIME_PROTECTIVE_DISTANCE_AND_GRAVITY_SCOPE_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-24.md) distinguish **DOC-CONFIRMED** ANSI publisher scope and ISO 2024 preview from SICK's 2021 ANSI B11.19-2019 example and Pilz's service-specific ten-measurement procedure. The ISO 2024 scope excludes gravity fall, mechanical failure and emissions; distance derived under that standard does not apply to safeguards used solely for presence sensing. The 2024 edition includes foot-operated *single* controls and inside-space SRMCD placement, but full normative calculations were not available. **INFERENCE:** one `SD-CASE` per hazardous movement/approach route must trace end-to-end sensing/logic/final-element/physical stop, reach-around/under/over, inside occupancy, and independent `PHY-HOLD/ENERGY/RESTART`. Ten trials or a single PLC stop bit do not certify worst-case physical stopping or a gravity load. All selected `IN/PHY/VAL/PT UNKNOWN/NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED; no numeric distance, physical test or personnel-exposed release. Historical exact archive remains **6/85** pending.


**11AU 2026-10-29 manufacturer source-depth (not a new safety function):** [Existing ISO 13855:2024 audit addendum](../research/4400_ISO13855_2024_SEPARATION_DISTANCE_SCOPE_STOP_TIME_AND_GRAVITY_EXCEPTION_2026-10-24.md) directly inspects Pilz's current explanatory `S=K×T+D_DS+Z` and dynamic `+S_M` formulation. `S_M` represents danger-point travel during the protective response; `Z` includes application/measurement/wear considerations. This is **DOC-CONFIRMED manufacturer explanation**, not inspected normative ISO clauses or a selected-machine calculation. Add to `SD-CASE`: independently evidenced moving-hazard trajectory, total response time, reach supplement, uncertainty/wear allowance and edition/type-C selection. Do not infer any term from ordinary LinuxCNC logs or claim a ten-run service series covers every mode/load. Physical `VAL/PT` still NOT RUN; G0/G1 HOLD.

### 11AV — Stop-time measurement purpose and manufacturer outlier rule are not transferable (2026-10-24)

[Rockford SSC3000 KSL252 source trace](../research/4400_SSC3000_DOWNSTROKE_DISTANCE_VS_UPSTROKE_BRAKE_MONITOR_SOURCE_TRAP_2026-10-24.md) and [technician exercise](4400_STOP_TIME_OUTLIER_AND_BRAKE_MONITOR_SCOPE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) inspect the 2025 OEM mechanical part-revolution clutch/brake press manual. **DOC-CONFIRMED:** downstroke STM is used for point-of-operation separation distance; upstroke STM near top stop is used for brake-monitor warning/fault settings. Its advice about disregarding unusually high readings appears in the *upstroke monitor-setting* procedure, not a general rule authorizing deletion of a credible long stop from a safety-distance validation. **INFERENCE:** independently witness actual hazardous motion, preserve every credible long stop and classify instrument-invalid readings with evidence; do not transfer crankshaft angle/percentages or OSHA 1910.217 mechanical-press requirements to hydraulic press brakes. `STM-DISTANCE/STM-BRAKE-MONITOR/STM-FAULT` paper-only; `PHY/VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED.


### 11AW — Bosch IH04/IH04E physical leakage and valve-switch source-scope boundary (2026-10-24)

The general IH04 load-side measuring-port 5 mm/s statement and IH04E die-cushion 1/5 mm/s statements are **different OEM-specific conditions**, not transferable machine safety thresholds. `OFF` may retain pressure in some lines. `BND/UNK`: IH04E is a lower die cushion, not a selected Y1/Y2 press-brake beam; ISO 6909:2026 public scope cannot fill missing normative clauses. Selected `IN-01..10/OPT/PHY UNKNOWN`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. No person-exposed operation without independent physical safeguards.


### 11AX — HAWE ePRAX: gravity-assisted downstroke, safety valve authority and optional accumulator energy (2026-10-24)

[HAWE B 6340 04-2026 source trace](../research/4400_HAWE_EPRAX_2026_GRAVITY_VALVE_ACCUMULATOR_SAFETY_SOURCE_TRACE_2026-10-24.md) and [field exercise](4400_HAWE_EPRAX_GRAVITY_VALVE_STORED_ENERGY_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) provide a **manufacturer-hosted real CNC press-brake** example. `AUTH/ARC`: normal per-axis servo-pump speed/torque and CNC synchronization are distinct from machine-builder safety-related hydraulic valve dropout and monitored beam speed. `DEP/PHY`: gravity accelerates FAST DOWN, so loss of drive speed control can remove normal hydraulic throttling; independent final elements and actual beam stop/hold are required. `ENE/PHY`: optional Slow Up accumulators can store hydraulic energy and raise the beam even without pump power; inventory variants/pressure zones. `CHG/VAL`: OEM orifice/measurement-coupling restrictions depend on beam weight/cylinder geometry/pressure; never borrow published numeric limits. `BND/UNK`: this is a **different** architecture from Bosch IH04E die cushion and neither is identified on OpenPressBrake. `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED; no exposed operation authorized.


**11AX option-code addendum (HAWE D 6340 07-2026):** newer OEM datasheet `https://productfinder.hawe.com/downloads/D6340-en.pdf` explicitly differentiates cylinder-module **`S` monitored-valve** option from **no monitoring**, and Slow Up **`X` absent** from **`A/B` accumulator variants**. `DEP/AUTH/ENE` must be qualified by **actual left/right installed order codes, electrical channels and hydraulic drawings**, not by an example product image or a generic variant circuit. B 6340 04-2026 and D 6340 07-2026 are distinct revisions; reconcile before `ARC/VAL/PT` credit. Selected machine remains UNKNOWN.


**11AX cross-family adversarial extension:** HAWE SAKB `S` monitored-valve coverage (D 6335) and ePRAX modular `S` coverage (D 6340) are **not identical**; ePRAX integrates normally-closed anti-cavitation while SAKB documentation separately identifies NSVs. B 6340's conditional safety-linear-scale/orifice design alternative is **not** a permission to remove physical restrictions based on normal CNC position readback. Intake now explicitly requires `IN-04a/IN-03a/IN-10a` complete option/feedback/revision evidence per Y axis. `BND/DEP/ARC/VAL UNKNOWN` until actual installed facts; no exposed release.


### 11AY — HAWE SAKB / NSV `L` seal suffix and `050/060` A/B port reversal (2026-10-24)

Y1–Y2 checks connected actuator NC contacts, not actual ram holding. This is documentary source evidence, **not** selected OpenPressBrake relay identity or power-fault testing. `IN-03/03a/04/04a/05/06/10/10a UNKNOWN`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. ### 11BB — Hydraulic pinhole injection, trapped hose pressure and safe service access (2026-10-24)

[Manufacturer/HSE injection and hose-failure source audit](../research/4400_HYDRAULIC_PINHOLE_INJECTION_HOSE_FAILURE_ISOLATION_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md) and [eight-case paper technician card](4400_HYDRAULIC_PINHOLE_LEAK_AND_PRESSURE_ZONE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) add a **separate high-pressure jet/injection/fitting-ejection hazard** to the ram crush/load-hold case. **DOC-CONFIRMED:** Parker 4400-B.1 §5.6 (2024) warns that pinholes may be hard to see, touching to find leaks is dangerous, and check valves may retain hose pressure after pump shutdown; failed hoses must be replaced, not patched. HSE FOD 4-2014 establishes the need for urgent medical care even after a small-looking injection wound. Enerpac warns against forcing coupler check balls/pins to release trapped thermal pressure. `BND/HAZ/ENE`: map every pressurized hose/accumulator/chamber and accessible isolation/bleed point, not only a global pump state. `PHY-ENERGY` requires independent zone-specific verification; a supported ram does not eliminate injection exposure. `HF/ARC`: place protected test points, service valves and restraints where correct use is easier than hand-probing, cracking fittings or removing guards. `CHG/VAL/PT`: hose/fitting substitution or routing changes invalidate dependent evidence. `IN-04/04a/07/08/09/10a UNKNOWN`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED; no generic safe pressure threshold or machine test claimed.


### 11BC — Pneumatic safe-exhaust application scope, connector variant and trapped-volume witness (2026-10-24)

[Manufacturer source audit](../research/4400_PNEUMATIC_SAFE_EXHAUST_VARIANT_RESET_TRAPPED_VOLUME_SCOPE_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md) and [ten-case paper field card](4400_PNEUMATIC_EXHAUST_TRAPPED_AIR_AND_RESET_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) extend `BND/ENE/HAZ/SF/PHY/AUTH/DEP/ARC/HF/CHG/VAL/PT` to auxiliary pneumatic/vacuum circuits in cells, clamps, saws and feeders. **DOC-CONFIRMED:** Festo 2023/05 MS6-SV-E/D catalogue forbids their use as press safety valves, constrains downstream components and exhaust backpressure, and distinguishes NECA MP3 from MP5 short/cross-circuit detection ownership; ROSS DM1 versus DM2 C fault memory/reset differs, and ROSS excludes Series C from press clutch/brake use. OSHA 1910.147 separately requires physical isolation and stored-energy verification during covered servicing. **INFERENCE:** an exhausted upstream valve does not prove a downstream check-isolated chamber is pressure-free or that a gravity/spring/vacuum load is retained. `PHY` must witness the actual protected volumes **and load**, `AUTH` keeps independent safety logic and physical final elements separate from LinuxCNC/FPGA diagnostics, and `CHG` invalidates evidence on connector, valve, silencer or service-unit substitution. Optional `IN-04c` as-built pneumatic intake remains **UNKNOWN**; `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. This is not an installed OpenPressBrake pneumatic architecture claim or machine approval.


**11BC vertical-axis physical-proposition addendum:** [Festo pages 49, 52 and 56](../research/4400_VERTICAL_PNEUMATIC_HOLD_VS_EXHAUST_FESTO_SOURCE_RECONCILIATION_2026-10-24.md) document a controlled vertical-axis stop/hold concept that **retains compressed air** and a distinct two-chamber pressurised holding example. The manufacturer says clamping/end-position locks are **not complete safety solutions**. Therefore `PHY-MOTION`, `PHY-HOLD`, `PHY-ENERGY`, `PHY-ACCESS` and `PHY-RESTART` must be separately traced; safe exhaust is not automatically safe holding, and trapped-pressure holding is not service LOTO. Standstill-before-clamp, final-element delay and feedback must be allocated to an independent qualified safety architecture rather than copied into LinuxCNC/FPGA. Actual selected pneumatic existence, restraint and test criteria UNKNOWN; no `VAL/PT` performed.


### 11BD — Hose-burst velocity fuse is conditional excess-flow protection, not a universal load hold (2026-10-24)

[HYDAC RB / HIDROS VUBA / Winner HB-18A manufacturer audit](../research/4400_HOSE_BURST_VELOCITY_FUSE_FLOW_WINDOW_AND_UNSAFE_TEST_TRANSFER_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md) and [ten-case technician card](4400_HOSE_BURST_FUSE_LOW_FLOW_AND_HAZARDOUS_TEST_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) add a distinct `DEP/PHY/ENE/HAZ/HF/CHG/VAL/PT` dependency: a flow-triggered fuse can only be credited for a **specified excess-flow failure class** with actual directional, installation and normal/fault flow-window evidence. **DOC-CONFIRMED:** HYDAC requires flow separation, warns against normal-cycle closure, describes pressure-dependent leakage and a dangerous physical burst-test method; HIDROS says VUBA is not a load-holding valve; Winner selection guidance differs. **INFERENCE:** low-flow injection leak, minimum-load low rupture flow, wrong protected segment or bypass may defeat an unqualified claim. Do **not** perform an improvised line-open test. `PHY-STOP`, `PHY-HOLD`, `PHY-HOSE-INJECTION`, `PHY-ENERGY` and service LOTO remain distinct; ordinary LinuxCNC/FPGA status cannot substitute for physical witnesses. Conditional selected `IN-04d UNKNOWN`; `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. No machine-specific trip setting or numerical acceptance inferred.

### 11BH — Scanner-to-safety-controller signal selection and rear-zone restart (2026-10-24)

**New OEM integration trap, not selected-machine proof.** Original SICK S300 8010948/ZD76/2025-08-19 printed §3.2.8 (p.23), §7.10 (pp.88–89), §§4.3/7.13 and Fig.46: https://www.sick.com/media/docs/3/13/613/operating_instructions_s300_safety_laser_scanner_en_im0017613.pdf . [Detailed original audit](../research/4400_SICK_S300_EFI_RESTART_INTERLOCK_SIGNAL_SELECTION_AND_REAR_ZONE_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md), [16-case paper card](4400_S300_EFI_SIGNAL_SELECTION_RESTART_AND_REAR_ACCESS_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md), [blank integration worksheet](4400_REAR_ACCESS_AND_EFI_RESTART_SAFETY_INTERFACE_REVIEW_WORKSHEET_2026-10-24.md).

**DOC-CONFIRMED:** scanner internal restart interlock is effective in Flexi Soft through **OSSD status**; when Flexi Soft instead consumes **protective-field status**, the internal scanner interlock does not apply to that controller path and must be implemented in Flexi Soft. SICK states that access fields do not detect people *behind* the protective plane, scanner mounting can leave unprotected areas, universal I/O is non-safe, and monitoring-case switching must be timed before exposure. **INFERENCE:** a field-clear / raw-status safety-PLC path without its own interlock can prematurely permit restart; exact selected circuit must be traced. **UNKNOWN:** OpenPressBrake scanner existence, PLC logic, backgauge STO/brake, rear occupancy, independent Y1/Y2 stop/hold and restart.

Trace `BND-REAR → HAZ-BACKGAUGE/ENTRAPMENT → SF-ACCESS/STOP/RESTART → ARC-SENSOR-SIGNAL → DEP-PLC-INTERLOCK → PHY-STOP/HOLD/OCCUPANCY → VAL/PT → CHG`. Preserve **which signal** the safety PLC consumes, **where reset authority resides**, **which final elements stop**, and **how a separate deliberate new start is enforced**. Front laser/ram stop != backgauge stop != safe rear occupancy; STO != physical gravity holding. Any scanner/field/PLC signal-selection change makes affected evidence STALE pending revalidation. Conditional `IN-04g UNKNOWN`; `EF-01..16 PAPER ONLY`; G0/G1 HOLD, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, no live person-in-field trial, no machine numerical acceptance or personnel-exposed operation.

### 11BI — S3000 EFI unallocated-field fail-open semantics and external OSSD bit trap (2026-10-24)

**Original SICK OEM 8009942/ZE09/2026-09-09**, official PDF https://www.sick.com/media/docs/3/63/863/operating_instructions_s3000_safety_laser_scanner_en_im0011863.pdf , printed pp.20, 93, 98–101. [Source audit](../research/4400_SICK_S3000_EFI_UNALLOCATED_FIELD_CLEAR_AND_OSSD_BIT_INTEGRATION_TRAPS_2026-10-24.md) and [ten-case paper card](4400_S3000_UNALLOCATED_FIELD_AND_EXTERNAL_OSSD_SIGNAL_TRAPS_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-24.md). **DOC-CONFIRMED:** an unallocated protective field initially reports **1 = clear**; SICK instructs transferring unallocated fields as interrupted for Flexi Soft field-status evaluation. If S3000 local OSSDs are not used, its EFI OSSD bit is **always active** and is **not** safety-authoritative. Dual protective field 2 does not switch local OSSDs; independent external cut-off paths are required. Warning fields are not personnel protection. This **qualifies** §11BH: scanner OSSD-status use is not universally valid; the configured signal's actual semantics must be verified before selecting interlock ownership. `CHG/ARC/DEP/SRS/PHY/VAL/PT` requires exact scanner family/edition, allocated fields, mode, EFI signal, local/external OSSD routing, PLC-side interlock, final-element and physical proof. Actual OpenPressBrake configuration **UNKNOWN**, `UF-01..10 PAPER ONLY`, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED.

**§11BI timing addendum:** S3000 2026 OEM §4.3.3 pp.39–40 and §7.8.2 pp.97–98 add `DEP-FIELD-SWITCH`: delayed field switching via input delays/external EFI control/external OSSDs can expose a person already in the newly activated field. Manufacturer describes advance timing and, when needed, field overlap or simultaneous monitoring; complementary and 1-of-n input fault semantics differ. **Do not** copy OEM timing increments into an unverified press-brake. `UF-11..13 PAPER ONLY`; selected timing, field geometry and safety input path UNKNOWN.


### 11BJ — Brownfield CNC replacement: independent safety-function continuity and service isolation (2026-10-24)

[Original OSHA/Pilz/LinuxCNC source audit](../research/4400_BROWNFIELD_CNC_RETROFIT_SAFETY_PLC_CUTOVER_AND_LOCKABLE_ISOLATION_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md), [12-case paper card](4400_RETAINED_SAFETY_PLC_RETROFIT_CUTOVER_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md), [blank cutover worksheet](4400_CNC_RETROFIT_SAFETY_FUNCTION_CONTINUITY_AND_ISOLATION_WORKSHEET_2026-10-24.md). A legacy safety PLC, unchanged checksum, HMI READY or `SAFETY_OK` FPGA input **does not** establish continued independent personnel-protective final-element withdrawal after ordinary CNC replacement. Per `BND/HAZ/ENE → SF/SRS → AUTH → ARC/DEP → PHY → VAL/PT → CHG`, reconcile before/after guard/E-stop inputs, PLC program and handshake, final-element wiring, supply dependencies, physical stop/hold/occupancy, local reset, stale ordinary start and maintenance isolation. **SOURCE-CONFIRMED** pinned LinuxCNC `estop_latch.comp` is a software latch; its reset edge and watchdog are not a safety PLC reset or physical stop witness. **DOC-CONFIRMED** OSHA 1910.147(c)(2)(iii) imposes lockout-capable energy-isolator design when qualifying major replacement/repair/renovation/modification occurs; 1910.302(c) addresses parallel electrical disconnect requirements. Do not automatically label any small software change a major modification or transfer EU CE duties into a US installation. Pilz's March 2024 retrofit is a distinct **eccentric press line**, not selected hydraulic press-brake proof. `IN-04h UNKNOWN`; all old/new wiring and physical witnesses UNKNOWN; `RC-01..12 PAPER ONLY`; `VAL/PT NOT RUN`; `G0/G1 HOLD`, `G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED`. No new machine-specific PL/SIL, stop time, pressure or physical test. **Freeze: RETAINED SAFETY PLC != RETAINED SAFETY FUNCTION; SAFETY_OK != PHYSICAL FINAL-ELEMENT AUTHORITY.**

### 11BM — Common actuator return, reverse-current false-high and controller brownout (2026-10-24)

[Original SICK Flexi Soft source audit](../research/4400_SICK_FLEXISOFT_LOST_0V_REVERSE_CURRENT_COMMON_CAUSE_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md) and [12-case paper technician card](4400_LOST_GROUND_FALSE_HIGH_AND_POWER_DIP_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) add `ARC-SUPPLY/RETURN`, `DEP-COMMON-GND`, `DEP-SENSOR-REVERSE-CURRENT` and `CHG-PSU/WIRING`. **DOC-CONFIRMED** SICK 8012478 Flexi Soft instructions §6.1: common actuator 0 V loss with mixed Q high/low can unintentionally switch a relay; parallel safety inputs can read high via reverse current. SICK Safe Linear Positioning 8020941 §13.2 gives XTIO hardware-version-dependent reverse-current maxima 20 mA (<V1.10) versus 2 mA (≥V1.10), not universal trip thresholds. The OEM also distinguishes logic/output supplies and warns about supply dips, shared encoder reference and ground loops. **INFERENCE:** a healthy safety-logic/HMI indication may coexist with a compromised final-element path. Conditional selected `IN-04k` requires actual as-built controller/module/hardware, PSU/return/FE/PE, input and actuator return junctions, cross-board supplies, independent `PHY-STOP/HOLD/ENERGY/RESTART`. Actual machine facts UNKNOWN, `LG-01..12 PAPER ONLY`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. A live 0 V disconnection experiment is **not** a student lab.

### 11BN — FX3-XTIO test-pulse disable can degrade other outputs and extend fault response (2026-10-24)

[Original SICK XTIO source audit](../research/4400_SICK_XTIO_DISABLED_TEST_PULSE_ALL_OUTPUTS_DEGRADE_AND_TRANSIENT_HIGH_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md). **DOC-CONFIRMED OEM-specific:** disabling Q1–Q4 test pulses on one or more FX3-XTIO outputs reduces safety parameters of **all** Q outputs of that module; SICK requires protected/separate wiring and a product-specific periodic off/restart measure. FX3-XTIO V3 extended higher-capacitance fault detection changes the manufacturer's LOW-threshold allowance from 3 ms to 43 ms and internal fault detection-plus-reaction from ≤10 ms to ≤50 ms in specified configurations; internal faults may briefly switch a nominally low single-channel Q high. **These are not physical machine stop-time acceptance values.** Supply-switching a PLC/FPGA output card with capacitors is not automatically a qualified safety final element. Selected `IN-04l` requires exact firmware, output pulse setting, load/capacitance, suppressed coil, cable, independent final element and `VAL/PT` revalidation after change. `TP-01..08 PAPER ONLY`, installed hardware UNKNOWN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. Normal LinuxCNC/FPGA remains ordinary control.

Beckhoff states the cited TwinCAT 2 SIL selection is unsupported and has **no safety behavior effect**. Conditional selected `IN-04m` must identify protocol, actual safety project, address, timeout, rearm, qualified output, physical final element and `PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS/ENERGY/RESTART` witnesses. **UNKNOWN** any selected OpenPressBrake safety network or physical stop. `FB-01..10 PAPER ONLY`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. ### 11BP — Cable-pull emergency-stop tension margin, diagnostic path and full-span reset (2026-10-24)

[Original Rockwell Lifeline 5/Lifeline 4 and Schmersal ZQ 900 audit](../research/4400_CABLE_PULL_ESTOP_TENSION_MARGIN_GUARDLINK_STANDARD_TAG_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md), [12-case paper technician card](4400_CABLE_PULL_TENSION_WARNING_RESET_SIGHTLINE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-24.md) and [blank selected-machine witness worksheet](4400_CABLE_PULL_ESTOP_INSTALLATION_CHANGE_AND_RESTART_WITNESS_WORKSHEET_2026-10-24.md) extend `BND/HAZ/SF/SRS → ARC-ROPE/RESET/SAFETY/DIAG → DEP → PHY-STOP/HOLD/ACCESS → VAL/PT → CHG`. **DOC-CONFIRMED OEM-specific:** Lifeline 5 high/low tension warnings can leave OSSD outputs **ON** (440E-IN008H February 2026 printed p.5 tables 6/7); 432ES GuardLink device tags/`Data` are **standard non-safety-rated** (pp.9–10), not an ordinary LinuxCNC safety input. Five-pin OSSD/GuardLink conductor semantics differ, eight-pin is OSSD-only (p.3). Device local reset/remote recoverable fault reset is not safety PLC rearm, occupancy proof or fresh start; full cable visibility or before/after inspection is required by the cited OEM. For any selected cable switch, require full-span access/pull direction, route/anchors/spring, exact catalog/firmware/pin mode, safe controller/final element, separate warning owner and independent physical `PHY` witnesses; record `CHG` and stale validation on rope/device/wiring changes. **No installed cable switch asserted**; conditional `IN-04n UNKNOWN`, `CP-01..12 PAPER ONLY`, `VAL/PT NOT RUN`, `G0/G1 HOLD`, `G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED`. Device 60 ms response or product PL labels **must not** be used as full machine stop time/rating. **Freeze: TENSION WARNING + OSSD ON != EFFECTIVE FULL-SPAN E-STOP; STANDARD GUARDLINK TAG != SAFETY AUTHORITY; RESET != START.**

### 11CK — Mechanical slide restraint: indicated engagement, physical engagement, load path, powered-energy isolation (2026-10-24)
**Source:** [OSHA 2007 original slide-lock directive audit](../research/4400_OSHA_SLIDE_LOCK_GRAVITY_VS_POWERED_STROKE_AND_PRESS_BRAKE_TRANSFER_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md); [4400-BLK-01 non-graduated module](4400_BLK_01_GRAVITY_RESTRAINT_DYNAMIC_FORCE_AND_LOTO_MODULE_2026-10-24.md). **Status:** documentary methodology, NOT selected-machine physical proof.

- **ENE/HAZ/PHY:** identify gravity load, tooling/attachments, foreseeable settling, powered hydraulic force, trapped pressure, service access and multiple persons. Never equate an engaged support designed for gravity with resistance to a powered stroke.
- **AUTH/ARC:** normal LinuxCNC/FPGA may request inhibit; independent safety and qualified isolators prevent hazardous powered energy; a properly selected and physically engaged mechanical support restrains gravity. The machine's actual design must justify every load case.
- **DEP:** record support sensor and visual witness common dependencies, structural anchor/frame, pin and fastener integrity, operator access, lock ownership and bypass incentive. Green lamp/limit bit is not an independent structural witness.
- **SRS/VAL/PT:** selected OEM design/rating, physical engagement and safe-location witness, electrical/hydraulic isolation and residual-energy verification, per-worker LOTO, initial/periodic inspection, change/incident retest, and deliberate fresh restart. All selected tests NOT RUN.
- **HF/CHG/RR/UNK:** inconvenient support storage, heavy handling, hidden engagement or hard-to-lock isolators create foreseeable omission. Any tooling mass, frame, support or interlock change makes related evidence STALE until requalified.
- **Scope:** OSHA CPL 02-01-043 Appendix's 2.0 factor is a *specific slide-lock performance guideline*, **not** a universal press-brake acceptance value. OSHA 1910.217 excludes press brakes; use applicable general guarding and hazardous-energy rules with selected-machine/OEM evidence.
- **Selected intake:** `IN-04aa UNKNOWN`. G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, G5 HOLD. No personnel-exposed operation.

### 11CW — Safety communication health is not ordinary bus health or physical machine safety (2026-10-24)
[Original Beckhoff/PI manufacturer and industry source audit](../research/4400_BECKHOFF_FSOE_VS_ORDINARY_ETHERCAT_WATCHDOG_REACTION_TIME_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-24.md), [NET-01 module](4400_NET_01_SAFETY_NETWORK_WATCHDOG_DUAL_LINK_REACTION_AND_PHYSICAL_BOUNDARY_MODULE_2026-10-24.md), [12 PAPER cases and blank witness](4400_SAFETY_NETWORK_TWO_WATCHDOG_TECHNICIAN_AND_SELECTED_WITNESS_2026-10-24.md). **DOC-CONFIRMED:** ordinary EtherCAT ESC SM/PDI watchdog and safety FSoE watchdog are distinct; documented ordinary SM=0 can disable ordinary fault reaction; FSoE has connection states, CRC/address diagnostics and configurable ComErrAck; worst-case two-link failure can consume two safety watchdog periods. **INFERENCE:** BUS OP, FSoE Data, safe-output FALSE or LinuxCNC E-stop are insufficient to prove physical Y1/Y2 gravity hold. Record BND ordinary bus versus certified safety logic; ARC exact OEM variant, version, safety signature, FSoE address and watchdog settings; DEP common power/network and two-link faults; AUTH safety-rated I/O/controller/final element independent of LinuxCNC/FPGA; PHY qualified actual terminal and gravity holding; VAL/PT protected measured worst-case stop/restart; CHG replacements, parameter download and zero watchdog; HF clear bus/safety/physical fault labels, easy no-bypass recovery. **NEVER INFER** `FAST ETHERCAT = FAST SAFETY STOP`, `SM WATCHDOG = FSoE WATCHDOG`, `FSoE DATA = PHYSICAL SAFE`, or `LINK RECOVERED = FRESH START`. Conditional selected **IN-04ah UNKNOWN** until installed-network inventory; G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED. No invented timing, SIL/PL or selected network presence.

No numeric support rating, machine compliance or exposure authorization.

**11EX original-source denominator clarification:** OSHA CPL 02-01-043 appendix §3.2/§4.1 defines a **slide-lock** maximum anticipated load (deadweight + foreseeable settling inertia) and a **minimum 2.0 design factor** relative to that load, conditional on the appendix's complete criteria; §§4.2/5 require personally securable engagement, control-reliable interlock/monitoring and two independent visual engagement indications. Cincinnati BASEFORM's **10% of machine forming capacity** is a different OEM **support-block** criterion. Do not compare/copy these numbers without selected device/load definition or infer powered-stroke survival. Source: [expanded audit](../research/4400_CINCINNATI_OSHA_RAM_BLOCK_STATIC_VS_POWERED_STROKE_AND_PRESS_BRAKE_SCOPE_AUDIT_2026-10-25.md); selected-machine PHY/VAL/PT still UNKNOWN/NOT RUN.

### 11HA — OFF-state electrical interface compatibility and physical-stop authority (2026-10-26; LEAK-01)
**SRS/ARC:** distinguish normal LinuxCNC/FPGA OFF command, output transistor/contact open, parallel RC or semiconductor leakage path, receiving input OFF threshold, physical final-element state and independent safety control. Rockwell IntelliVAC 1503-UM052D-EN-P (June 2013) printed pp. 1-6, 3-12–3-13, 5-2 is **DOC-CONFIRMED for its exact interface**: residual OFF leakage can maintain CLOSE, two-wire Power-Up Safety may be disabled, TDUV may delay opening, and its Contactor Status relay may assert during a 200 ms command interval rather than solely after physical closure. Schmersal AES 2335 UE 101180843 minimum switching rating 10 VDC/10 mA is a **separate exact-product** constraint. Do not transfer either product's electrical limits or claimed safety rating to OpenPressBrake.
**DEP/CHG:** on a change of output, receiver, suppression, contactor or module revision, reopen OFF-leakage, minimum contact-load, inductive transient, delayed drop-out, power return, status-vs-auxiliary feedback and deliberate fresh rearm. A generic interposing relay is not by itself independent safety authority. Require qualified OEM-compatible interface and monitored final elements.
**PHY/VAL:** independently witness actual Y1/Y2 stop/hold, hydraulic pressure-zone isolation, guarding/access, residual energy and restart only after G0/G1 documentation. Do not run personnel-exposed trials or bypass safety devices. Selected as-built device, wiring, thresholds, stop time and PL/SIL **UNKNOWN**; G0/G1 HOLD, G2 NOT RUN, G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED, G5 HOLD. [Source audit](../research/4400_ROCKWELL_INTELLIVAC_OFF_STATE_LEAKAGE_SNUBBER_POWER_UP_AND_DELAY_OEM_AUDIT_2026-10-26.md), [learner module](4400_LEAK_01_OFF_COMMAND_LEAKAGE_MINIMUM_LOAD_AND_FRESH_REARM_MODULE_2026-10-26.md).

[Source](../research/4400_MACH_ACCESS_01_ISO14122_OSHA_INTEGRAL_LADDER_HSE_MAINTENANCE_ACCESS_SCOPE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md); [module](4400_MACH_ACCESS_01_INTEGRAL_LADDER_PLATFORM_AND_MAINTENANCE_OPENING_FIELD_MODULE_2026-10-27.md); [crosscut](4400_MACH_ACCESS_01_REMOVABLE_GRATING_ACCESS_AND_PHYSICAL_HANDBACK_CROSSCUT_2026-10-27.md). G0/G1 HOLD.

### 11IN — MACH-LOW-02 low-height fall into dangerous equipment and falling objects (2026-10-27)
**BND/HAZ:** low service step/platform, removed hole or dropped tools near dangerous machinery/people below. **DOC-CONFIRMED direct US eCFR 1910.28:** (b)(3)(ii) covers stepping/tripping through holes below four feet; (b)(6) covers falling into dangerous equipment below four feet; (c) addresses falling-object exposure. The general four-foot unprotected-edge rule does not erase these other hazards. **PHY:** cover/guard opening, prevent fall into hazardous machinery, protect people below; selected independent machine stop/hold still separate. **AUTH/DEP:** machine E-stop indication cannot prove fall/dropped-object protection. **HF/CHG:** practical service reach and tool handling, physical exclusion, reinspection after panel/access changes. **VAL/UNK:** six public paper cases UNSCORED, six desk gates NOT RUN, selected OpenPressBrake geometry/exposure UNKNOWN; zero compute. [Source](../research/4400_MACH_LOW_02_ECFR_1910_28_HOLES_DANGEROUS_EQUIPMENT_FALLING_OBJECT_SCOPE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md); [module](4400_MACH_LOW_02_BELOW_FOUR_FEET_DANGEROUS_EQUIPMENT_HOLES_AND_DROPPED_TOOLS_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-27.md). ## 11IO. DUST-05 combustible particulate and collector connected-path witness (2026-10-27)

[DUST-05 predecessor correction](../research/4400_DUST_05_PREDECESSOR_OVERLAP_RECONCILIATION_AND_SOURCE_DELTA_2026-10-27.md). **Applicability:** conditional to a machine/process producing or handling potentially combustible fine particulates; no evidence that OpenPressBrake does so. **BND/ENE/HAZ:** inventory material, particle-generating process, oxidizer, ignition sources, collector/hopper/duct/return-air/room confinement and people. A primary event can resuspend deposits and cause secondary propagation (CSB incident DOC-CONFIRMED); selected process UNKNOWN. **SF/SRS:** separate normal extraction/air-quality control from material-specific ignition management, qualified collector vessel protection, upstream/downstream duct isolation, vent discharge safety and maintenance energy control. **PHY:** `DUST-PHY-01` material characterized; `-02` collector protected; `-03` connected paths isolated; `-04` vent discharge safe; `-05` deposits controlled; `-06` maintenance safe. All UNKNOWN until selected-machine evidence. **AUTH:** LinuxCNC/FPGA fan/spindle command, airflow/filter HMI and E-stop are ordinary control/diagnostic, not independent fire/explosion protection or physical safe-state proof. **DEP/ARC:** selected active/passive protective devices, shared supply/ground, detector, final element, duct geometry, vent direction, housekeeping and service access; product-specific design required. **HF:** make correct cleaning, inspection, guard restoration and isolation easier than bypassing nuisance alarms or blowing dust into the air. **CHG:** dust/material/process, collector, duct, vent, filter, protective system, maintenance method or occupancy change stales affected `PHY/VAL/PT`. **VAL/PT/UNK:** 10 public paper cases UNSCORED, 12 documentary gates NOT RUN; physical qualification, material properties, selected protection and independent evaluator UNKNOWN/NOT RUN. No explosive lab; no compute. [Source audit](../research/4400_DUST_05_OSHA_CSB_FIKE_NFPA660_COLLECTOR_PROPAGATION_SOURCE_DELTA_2026-10-27.md), [field module](4400_DUST_05_PRIMARY_SECONDARY_COLLECTOR_TRANSFER_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-27.md), [CCF/change review](4400_DUST_05_COLLECTOR_ISOLATION_CCF_CHANGE_TRANSFER_REVIEW_2026-10-27.md). **G0/G1 HOLD; no personnel-exposed hazardous operation.**

### 11IT — OpenPressBrake retained-Pilz handshake, mute and switched-return composition (2026-10-27)
[Exact Rev 1 source/config audit](../research/4400_OPB_CUTOVER_02_PILZ_MUTE_GATE_POWER_DOMAIN_CROSS_REPOSITORY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) → [12-case technician desk review](4400_OPB_CUTOVER_02_PILZ_MUTE_GATE_POWER_DOMAIN_FIELD_REVIEW_2026-10-27.md) → [restricted Stage B delta](4400_OPB_CUTOVER_02_RESTRICTED_HANDSHAKE_AND_POWER_DELTA_CARD_2026-10-27.md). **This is a design-source crosswalk, not as-built validation.**

| Package ID | Exact project interface / claim | Narrow evidence and disposition |
|---|---|---|
| `BND/HAZ-OPB-02` | Y1/Y2 press, X drive, guarding/muting, restart, maintenance | Machine hazard map and modes still UNKNOWN; G0 HOLD. |
| `AUTH-OPB-02A` | Normal DNC replacement sends wires 42–47 into Pilz, including MUTE 44/CNC OK 45 | SOURCE-CONFIRMED *published Rev 1 endpoint map*; installed Pilz trust, polarity, freshness and qualification UNKNOWN. |
| `AUTH-OPB-02B` | Pilz wire 75 proportional inhibit; wire 63 direct X-drive enable; Pilz-owned 11Y4/11Y6/11Y8 | Design-intended separation SOURCE-CONFIRMED; independent final-element effect and hydraulic safe state NOT PROVED. |
| `DEP-OPB-02` | 11K2 two-pole L7/L07, possible L07–L06 bypass, PVR X2 high-current return, duplicated wire 111 | Source-map warnings confirmed; actual physical wiring, shared return and fault behavior UNKNOWN. |
| `SRS/PHY-OPB-02` | Independently justified guard/mute, Y1/Y2 STOP/HOLD, X stop, pressure/energy, deliberate restart | Selected safety program, physical witnesses and acceptance criteria UNKNOWN; no generic truth table, timing, PL/SIL or pressure inferred. |
| `VAL/PT-OPB-02` | Review stuck MUTE/CNC OK, wire 75 bypass, switched-return bridge, safety-valve migration and held restart | Twelve documentary gates NOT RUN; physical test NOT AUTHORIZED without G1 isolated/remote plan. |
| `CHG/HF-OPB-02` | Connector substitution, guard bypass incentives, fault message clarity, service return/rewire | Any changed wire or program semantics makes dependent evidence STALE; safe, keyed, inspectable wiring is easier than bypass. |

**Contradiction rule:** Pilz status/valve feedback healthy but physical STOP/HOLD/pressure/guard witness disagrees ⇒ `PHY NOT ESTABLISHED`, `VAL/DEP/CHG` reopen, G0/G1 HOLD, no rearm or personnel exposure. This crosswalk cannot promote source-design claims to TEST-CONFIRMED machine evidence.


**11IT implementation-source precedence:** [Current schema v3 and quarantined-netlist audit](../research/4400_OPB_CUTOVER_02_MAX22216_FOUR_INPUT_GATE_FANOUT_AND_STALE_AUTHORITY_RECONCILIATION_2026-10-27.md) narrows `AUTH/ARC/DEP-OPB-02`: ordinary hardware permit = four prerequisites, wire 75 has distinct hardware and diagnostic consumers, and permit reaches two MAX22216 ENABLE pins. The older `ENABLE_PATH_HEALTHY` fifth term is circular/stale; path health is a validation concern, not a Boolean input. `VAL/PT` requires exact field conditioning, schematic connectivity, power-off/boot behavior, fresh-command recovery and independent machine physical witness; **all NOT RUN/UNKNOWN**. Current safety-interface block is NOT SCHEMATIC-READY. No safety-function or physical STOP/HOLD credit.

### 11JC — OPB-ISO-03 switched-field isolator diagnostic validity and FLT1/FLT2 source precedence (2026-10-27)
[Direct OEM and public design-source audit](../research/4400_OPB_L7_L07_ISOLATOR_POWER_LOSS_FAULT_DIAGNOSTIC_POLARITY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) → [field reviewer](4400_OPB_ISO_03_SWITCHED_FIELD_POWER_AND_DIAGNOSTIC_VALIDITY_REVIEW_CARD_2026-10-27.md). **BND/AUTH:** ordinary STISO621/STISO620 isolation, IPS1025H output diagnostics, 11K2 L7/L07 switching and FPGA fault bits are not personnel-safety final elements; independent Pilz/AKAS and hydraulic stop/hold remain authoritative. **DEP/DIAG:** when field TX is lost while logic RX remains powered, STISO620 drives LOW only after watchdog, which may be invalid not healthy; when receiver itself is unpowered STISO620 is TRI-STATE and STISO621 is UNKNOWN. IPS1025H VCC-loss and GND-loss differ. **SOURCE-CONFLICT:** Rev1 prose swaps FLT1/FLT2 semantic labels relative to ST manufacturer and later Rev2/manifest; resolve final physical netlist, FPGA/HMI polarity, field-power-valid status and staleness before using diagnostics. **PHY/VAL:** actual 11K2 poles, returns/cross-feeds, safety program, independent Y1/Y2 stop/hold, guard and restart remain UNKNOWN/NOT RUN. **HF:** easy field-power-invalid indication and non-bypass service workflow. **CHG:** final netlist, firmware, sensor, safety application, harness or power-domain change reopens affected evidence. This is exact OEM/source-depth and existing OPB-D06/D08 refinement, not a new scored competency, no test or compute. **G0/G1 HOLD.**

**11JC direct ST schematic source-depth correction:** ST X-NUCLEO-ISO1A1 Rev1 (March 2025) page 2 confirms STISO621's bidirectional command/FLT1 orientation. On switched-field power loss with logic alive, **FLT1/STISO621 and FLT2/STISO620** may both report LOW after their respective watchdogs, while the unpowered STISO621 field command RX is UNKNOWN. [Corrected original audit](../research/4400_OPB_L7_L07_ISOLATOR_POWER_LOSS_FAULT_DIAGNOSTIC_POLARITY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md); both diagnostics must be treated as INVALID without independent process-power validity. No physical or evaluator proof, G0/G1 HOLD.

**11JC additional DEP/DIAG/PT:** TI ISO1212 Rev G Table 7-2 directly says an open field IN/SENSE returns LOW while logic receiver powered; TI TIDA-01509 requires additional broken-wire detection hardware. Public optional `INPUT_VALID` and no explicitly named L7-valid DI cannot establish a physical power/wire-valid witness. A retained HIGH ordinary command may propagate again after field power returns (INFERENCE), requiring independently proven fresh start. [Original source audit](../research/4400_OPB_L7_L07_ISOLATOR_POWER_LOSS_FAULT_DIAGNOSTIC_POLARITY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [restricted D06/D08](4400_OPB_CUTOVER_02_RESTRICTED_HANDSHAKE_AND_POWER_DELTA_CARD_2026-10-27.md). No safety credit, test, PL/SIL or numerical stop/restart time; G0/G1 HOLD.

**11JC physical failure-path addendum:** Source-described 11K2 switches both L7 positive and L07 return. One-pole contact weld/cross-feed is not automatically equivalent to both-pole disconnection: IPS1025H VCC-loss and GND-loss have different OEM behavior. Actual contactor/EDM, alternate return and independent Pilz-controlled hydraulic final elements remain UNKNOWN; see [conditional source-specific matrix](../research/4400_OPB_L7_L07_ISOLATOR_POWER_LOSS_FAULT_DIAGNOSTIC_POLARITY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [existing restricted D06](4400_OPB_CUTOVER_02_RESTRICTED_HANDSHAKE_AND_POWER_DELTA_CARD_2026-10-27.md). No contact fault injection or personnel-exposed operation; G0/G1 HOLD.

**11JC practical transfer/diagnostic HMI caveat:** TI TIDA-01509 uses **intentional ISO121x field-ground interruption** to detect broken wires; that manufacturer reference cannot authorize an L07/Pilz shared-return pulse. Proposed ordinary VALID/INVALID/FAULT HMI semantics improve anti-defeat usability but do not qualify personnel safety. ST IPS1025H fault-current-source clamp versus STISO621 input maximum remains a bounded **UNKNOWN** complete-circuit/board-lane rating question, not a proven defect. [Exact audit](../research/4400_OPB_L7_L07_ISOLATOR_POWER_LOSS_FAULT_DIAGNOSTIC_POLARITY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md), [field card](4400_OPB_ISO_03_SWITCHED_FIELD_POWER_AND_DIAGNOSTIC_VALIDITY_REVIEW_CARD_2026-10-27.md). G0/G1 HOLD; no test or compute.


## 11JD — active safety-project identity, real-program source provenance and physical final-element proof (2026-10-27)
**Predecessors:** 2530 reset/restart, 2540 external-device monitoring, AKAS-M/AMS3, three-stage pedal, OPB-CUTOVER and OPB-ISO-03 already own generic functions. [Exact case-study/provenance audit](../research/4400_SAFETY_PROGRAM_IDENTITY_FPSC_EXAMPLE_PILZ_CRC_VALVE_FEEDBACK_AND_BACKFEED_SOURCE_REVIEW_2026-10-27.md) and [review card](4400_SAFE_PROGRAM_04_ACTIVE_PROJECT_AND_FINAL_ELEMENT_EVIDENCE_REVIEW_CARD_2026-10-27.md) add a *real implementation comparison*, not a new safety function or physical validation.

**BND/HAZ/SF/SRS:** an ordinary CNC OK/MUTE label is a process request, not automatically a safety permissive. **AUTH:** selected independent safety-controller active program must be identified on the installed hardware. **DOC-CONFIRMED Pilz PNOZ m B0** `1002660-EN-13` §§7.3–7.5/8.2.2.2 provides project name/CRC and function checks after project/card changes, **not** proof B0 is installed. **COMMUNITY-REPORTED** mirrored Fiessler FPSC 2006 example illustrates external valve-rest feedback, program readback and potential AKAS receiver-off FUO/FUS backfeed; exact legacy macros/terminals and the original PDF remain unverified. **SOURCE-CONFIRMED public OpenPressBrake Rev1** normal wire 44/45 labels are not the selected Pilz logic.

**ARC/DEP/DIAG:** program file on laptop ≠ active controller application; checksum match ≠ validated program; relay/valve auxiliary feedback ≠ gravity-load hold; reset ≠ deliberate new start. A missing field-power-valid signal may make LOW fault diagnostics untrustworthy (see 11JC). **VAL/PT:** qualified reviewer must match actual installed Pilz model, firmware, active program/export/CRC and each input/output to independently credited final elements, then separately validate physical Y1/Y2 stop/hold, isolation, guards, occupancy and fresh start. Existing restricted OPB-D01/D02/D03/D06/D08 covers this; do not duplicate intake. **HF:** make approved program identification and fault localization easy without bypass. **CHG/UNK:** controller replacement, program/chip-card changes, I/O remap, AKAS/valve replacement stale dependent evidence; all selected facts UNKNOWN. No new score, physical test, compute or G0/G1 release.

### 11JM — Powered movable guard: closing-edge protection versus enclosure protection (2026-10-27)
**Source:** [Haas powered-door edge, CAN current, and robot-fence removal audit](../research/4400_AUTODOOR_01_HAAS_CNC_POWERED_DOOR_EDGE_CURRENT_FENCE_REMOVAL_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [technician reviewer](4400_AUTODOOR_01_MOVING_GUARD_EDGE_AND_FENCE_REMOVAL_REVIEW_CARD_2026-10-27.md). This is a cross-machine documentary example, not a selected machine's safety proof.

- **BND/ENE/HAZ:** A motorized/pneumatic door has its *own* closing-edge nip/crush and falling-leaf/service hazards, separate from spindle/robot/projectile hazards contained by the enclosure. Map people on both sides and power-loss/maintenance states.
- **SF/PHY:** (1) prevent or safely arrest dangerous door closure; (2) keep machine hazards inaccessible while active; (3) control restart/occupancy; (4) isolate and physically support the leaf for service. Door-closed switch, edge INPUT 128, motor-current reversal, CAN alarm, cell-safe bit and ordinary M-code each witness only limited propositions; none alone proves all four.
- **AUTH/DEP:** Keep LinuxCNC/FPGA and ordinary door controller as request/diagnostic layers. Qualified safety-related logic, selected edge/interlock, guard locking and physical final elements retain their own authority. A fence, scanner, light curtain or edge may not be substituted without selected geometry/coverage/integrity analysis. Haas TG0044 CAN current-based reversal and TG0019 8M timed/closed-switch/P1405 hold are different ordinary-control generations; do not transfer fault response between them.
- **HF/CHG:** Haas documents a variant where robot/APL fence substitutes for an omitted edge sensor; removal of fenced automation **invalidates the old safeguard assumption**. Prefer accessible edge kits, protected cabling and simple alignment/maintenance over bypass. Treat guard removal, door rail/lock adjustment, software/option patch, motor change and cell-safe reconfiguration as CHG, staling affected VAL/PT.
- **VAL/PT:** Verify selected sensor electrical faults, physical door closing/reversal and guard stop, no automatic restart, egress, lock/rail alignment, door fall prevention and service isolation with a qualified non-exposing method. No hand-in-door testing. Exact stopping/force/PL/SIL and selected-machine evidence UNKNOWN/NOT RUN. G0/G1 HOLD.

**11JM selected-edge transfer gate:** Schmersal SE-400 C product specifies monitored edge/evaluator/feedback and up-to-PL-e, SE-100 C a different up-to-PL-c/Category-1 architecture, and SE-P40 manufacturer profile warns approximately 60 mm at each end where finger protection is not guaranteed. These are **DOC-CONFIRMED product-specific** examples; module reaction time ≠ physical door stopping time, component PL ≠ machine SF PL, sensor input changes ≠ end-zone protection. Validate selected sensor + evaluator + final element + door geometry and reset together; no cross-vendor Haas compatibility implied.

### 11JN — Passive viewing-window containment is independent of guard interlock and optical clarity (2026-10-27)
**Source:** [Original machine-window ageing/retention OEM audit](../research/4400_WINDOW_01_POLYCARBONATE_GUARD_WINDOW_AGEING_RETENTION_AND_REPLACEMENT_OEM_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [technician reviewer](4400_WINDOW_01_AGED_VIEWING_WINDOW_PROJECTILE_CONTAINMENT_REVIEW_CARD_2026-10-27.md). Documentary cross-machine example; selected machine and qualified physical validation UNKNOWN.
- **BND/HAZ/ENE:** Rotating chuck, tool or abrasive wheel can eject fragments into a transparent enclosure. Credible selected ejection threat/energy/trajectory and operator exposure require actual machine evidence; no numerical impact rating invented.
- **SF/PHY:** An interlocked door restricts access; it does **not** guarantee pane/frame projectile retention. Transparent polycarbonate may chemically age without visible clouding; laminated protection depends on intact edge seals and mounting. Separate pane identity, service date, chemical exposure, layer ingress, support frame/fasteners and correct impact qualification.
- **AUTH/DEP:** Normal LinuxCNC/FPGA maintenance counters or green interlock inputs cannot certify passive impact integrity. Safety-related interlock/final elements, mechanical containment, service isolation and restart remain independent propositions. Optical laser attenuation is another separate physical function.
- **HF/CHG:** Provide accessible OEM spare pane/installation kit, clear dated external label, approved cleaning instructions and easy guard reinstatement; do not create an incentive to run with expired pane. Changed coolant/cleaner, tooling/spindle speed, pane supplier/laminate, seal or frame stales affected physical witness. OEM two-year grinder and five-year protected-laminate recommendations are product-specific, not universal norms.
- **VAL/PT:** WIN-1 identity/date; WIN-2 threat; WIN-3 chemical/age/condition; WIN-4 qualified pane/frame retention; WIN-5 changes/interlock/restart; WIN-6 human factors. **All UNKNOWN/NOT RUN** for selected machine; no impact test or exposed operation authorized. ABRASIVE-01/GUARD/AUTODOOR/LASER-ENC retained as distinct predecessors. OpenPressBrake G0/G1 HOLD.

**11JN independent primary-source depth:** DGUV IFA 5169 (2022 project status) reported a one-year window with significantly reduced retention and unresolved causes, so even an unexpired pane cannot be accepted from age/clarity alone; historical 2–12-year intervals vary by construction/chemistry. DGUV/BIA 6054 historical 100-g projectile material comparison shows PMMA/acrylic and polycarbonate not interchangeable; do not import its impact speed/thickness as a machine design value. ISO 16090-1:2022 machining-centre publisher scope checked, normative clauses not inspected. Selected WIN-1..6 remain UNKNOWN.


### 11JO — Safety cable routing, EMC common-cause coupling and post-retrofit physical witness (2026-10-27)
**Source:** [Original Pilz/SICK/VACON/IEC installation audit](../research/4400_EMC_01_PILZ_SICK_VACON_SAFETY_WIRING_COUPLING_AND_INSTALLATION_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [field reviewer](4400_EMC_01_SAFETY_SIGNAL_CABLE_ROUTING_AND_EMC_CHANGE_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-27.md). - **SF/PHY:** Selected safety input must be correctly interpreted and independent final elements must actually stop/hold the hazard. Input LEDs, nominal voltage, FPGA diagnostics or compliant device datasheet do not prove physical ram stopping or gravity holding.
- **AUTH/DEP:** Pilz 1002265-EN-02 §§3/5 prohibits unprotected test-pulse/actuator multicore and defines product voltage-group spacing; SICK deTec4 2026 §§4.4/6 requires separate OSSDs and product-specific 0 V/M12 wiring; VACON 100 2015 §5.3 has different drive-specific motor cable spacing. **Never combine these numbers into a universal minimum.** Distinguish PE, 0 V, FE, shields, test-source and ordinary FPGA wiring.
- **HF/CHG:** Prefer accessible segregated marked ducts, shield clamps, keyed M12, cable labels and service drawings. Changed motor/drive, added coil, rerouted cable, replaced connector or shield termination creates CHG and stales dependent VAL/PT. Solve nuisance trips without bypass.
- **VAL/PT:** Conditional EMC-1 device/version, EMC-2 as-built route, EMC-3 reference/PE/shield, EMC-4 diagnostic faults, EMC-5 qualified non-exposing commissioning, EMC-6 restart/change. **All UNKNOWN/NOT RUN** for OpenPressBrake. No deliberate live faults or hazardous operation with people exposed. IEC 61000-6-7:2014/61326-3-1:2017 publisher scopes only; paid test criteria not inspected. G0/G1 HOLD.
