**Source:** [Original manufacturer/regulator scope and bypass audit](../research/4400_FIRE_SUP_01_CNC_AUTOMATIC_SUPPRESSION_PRESSURE_SWITCH_BYPASS_AGENT_REENTRY_SCOPE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) → [field card](4400_FIRE_SUP_01_CNC_SUPPRESSION_DISCHARGED_IMPAIRED_RESTART_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-27.md). No selected-machine validation.
- **BND/ENE/HAZ:** enclosed CNC with oil mist/hot tooling, possible combustible metal or other agent-specific hazards; discharge can create CO2/decomposition-product exposure in enclosure/adjacent low areas. Firetrace pneumatic tubing may discharge without ordinary CNC power; machine-specific selected agent and topology UNKNOWN.
- **SF/PHY:** separately prove detector coverage, agent delivery, process fuel/ignition withdrawal, independent motion stop/hold, safe atmospheric access and restored suppressor/restart. **Discharge switch != fire extinguished; fire extinguished != atmosphere safe; CNC E-stop != charged suppressor.**
- **AUTH/DEP:** optional pressure switch may request ordinary CNC/oil-pump/mist-collector stop; do not assign personnel-safety integrity to accessory contact, HAL or FPGA. Qualified independent machine safety remains separate. Fan/damper behavior must follow selected fire strategy, not generic all-ON/all-OFF.
- **HF/CHG:** Firetrace customer stories report one shop bypassing a pressure switch after discharge and another refusing; COMMUNITY-REPORTED only. Make correct spare/refill and qualified service faster/easier than bypass; conspicuous IMPAIRED state, separate deliberate restart. Changed coolant, metal, agent, tubing/nozzles, extraction or pressure-switch wiring stales dependent proof.
- **VAL/PT:** FS-1..6 exact selected agent/approval and fire/exposure scope, detector/discharge coverage, shutdown authority, impairment control, qualified service/inspection, atmosphere/re-entry/fresh start. **All UNKNOWN/NOT RUN.** OSHA §1910.160(a)(2) limited optional-system applicability and §1910.162 total-flooding distinction must be assessed, not assumed. No live fire, agent release, bypass, physical/evaluator or hosted/local compute. OpenPressBrake applicability UNKNOWN; G0/G1 HOLD.
**11JP exact OEM addendum:** Original Firetrace DIOM 800095-A Rev A (2022-07-22) §§2–4 provides keyed and standard dual-switch variants and 16 keyed switch configurations; some bypass activation **and** service, others bypass activation while **forcing service indication**. Thus `KEYED SERVICE BYPASS` is not universally an illicit wire jumper, but `KEYED BYPASS != SAFE AUTOMATIC PRODUCTION`. Selected FS-3/4 must record module/configuration, key custody, actual activation/service output states and downstream inhibition/annunciation. Qualified distributor consultation/recharge after discharge; 2020 TB041 is different. All selected-machine VAL/PT UNKNOWN; G0/G1 HOLD.

**11JP physical observability correction:** Firetrace DIOM 800095-A §3.7 end-of-line pressure gauge is on **pneumatic detection tubing**, not proof of agent-cylinder charge/nozzle discharge. Require separate `TUBE_PRESSURE_AND_COVERAGE`, `AGENT_INVENTORY`, `DISCHARGE_PATH` and `FIRE/ATMOSPHERE` witnesses; a single pressure-green bit cannot close all four. No selected values or tests.

**11JP exact selected-family scope:** Firetrace HFC-227ea ILP P/N 800022-010 (2016) explicitly describes **total flooding within normally unoccupied CNC-capable enclosure** and requires closing openings/shutting ventilation at discharge; **do not** infer all CNC suppressors are local-only or all room pre-discharge provisions apply. The same family excludes reactive metal fires (Mg/Ti/Zr), while an oil-coolant fire on a titanium workpiece is a distinct fuel. Preserve agent/fuel/enclosure/duct/room exposure and selected edition in FS-1..3; no generic pressure, concentration or fan timing adopted.

## 11JQ. 4400-CHIP-03 — CNC auxiliary conveyor software-version door rule is not physical isolation (2026-10-27; NON-GRADUATED)
**Predecessor:** CHIP-01 timed conveyor restart/jam reversal/LOTO, CHIP-02 LinuxCNC-vs-Haas M-code mapping. **New exact OEM evidence:** [CHIP-03 source delta](../research/4400_CHIP_03_HAAS_CONVEYOR_DOOR_OPEN_AUTO_START_SOFTWARE_REVISION_OEM_SOURCE_DELTA_2026-10-27.md) traces Haas service-manual software **100.17.000.1016** uncommanded auger/conveyor start **even with doors open**, OEM **100.17.000.2030+** corrective guidance, pre-fix M31 off-interval re-trigger and Classic-vs-NGC updated-after-2018-10-10 door differences. All are **DOC-CONFIRMED OEM text**, not a machine physical test or source-code proof. Intentional Settings 114/115 timed cycling persists in current product documentation; never treat the OEM's context-bound “never restarts automatically” as a universal rule.

| ID | Hazard / proposition | Ordinary control and diagnostics | Required independent witness | State |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BND-CHIP03 | Selected machine/controller family/option/software build/door geometry | OEM service table and firmware inventory can flag known issue | Dated installed revision + applicable OEM instructions + physical access survey | UNKNOWN |
| SF/PHY-CHIP03 | No hazardous conveyor/auger motion while a person accesses the chute/nip or clears jam | Door bit, M33, software patch and alarm are not a verified physical safe state | Qualified safety-path/final-element evidence and no-person-exposure proof, after readiness | NOT RUN |
| DEP/CHG-CHIP03 | Version change, Classic/NGC transfer, M31 re-trigger, manual reverse, timer cycle, power return | Control firmware behavior varies; no generic LinuxCNC M31/M33 assumption | Change-control baseline, hazard-dependent revalidation and isolation map | UNKNOWN |
| HF-CHIP03 | Frequent jams and awkward guard removal motivate defeat | Normal reversal may be available at pendant | Captive/hinged guard, reachable lockable isolator and usable safe cleanout verified with operator/maintainer | UNKNOWN |

**Authority:** ordinary CNC/HAL/FPGA may command/monitor, independent safety-related control and physical isolation must be qualified for the selected machine. OSHA 1910.147 includes hazardous cleaning/unjamming in maintenance scope; an OEM software fix is not a lockout. **No selected conveyor asserted on OpenPressBrake, no machine-specific PL/SIL, stopping time, test, evaluator or compute.** G0/G1 HOLD, no personnel-exposed hazardous operation.

## 11JR. 4400-TSC/TAB-01 — Two-media check valves, pressure sensor and safe service are separate propositions (2026-10-27; NON-GRADUATED)
**Predecessor:** HYD-INJ injection/hoses, ATC tool release, WJ pressure bleed, AIR-CLEAN ordinary output, CHIP auxiliary restart. **New exact source delta:** [original Haas TG0145 Rev C / AD0086 audit](../research/4400_TSC_TAB_01_HAAS_COOLANT_AIR_CHECK_VALVE_BACKFLOW_PRESSURE_WITNESS_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [field card](4400_TSC_TAB_01_COOLANT_AIR_BACKFLOW_AND_PRESSURE_SERVICE_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-27.md). Manufacturer **DOC-CONFIRMED** two opposite fault paths: failed TAB air check sends coolant into CALM air tubing/muffler; spring-and-ball coolant check can trap ball and send TAB air back into coolant tank, causing bubbling/overflow. Shuttle-and-ball variant has a slotted pressure-sensor elbow. AD0086 has separate pump, prime/bleed, relief and bypass with pressure dependent on tool opening. Pressure input, firmware alarm threshold and M88/M89 normal command do not prove physical isolation.

| ID | Hazard / physical proposition | Ordinary control or misleading observation | Required independent selected-machine witness | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BND-TSC01 | Actual CNC/pump/TSC/TAB/check-valve variant and firmware | Haas VF build window, TG0145 revision and diagnostic thresholds apply only to selected equipment | As-built option/valve/firmware and OEM edition; no OpenPressBrake applicability inferred | UNKNOWN |
| ENE-TSC02 | Two independent air and coolant energy sources, check valves, pressure-sensor tap, bleed/relief/bypass and trapped service volumes | TSC pressure or TAB status is not every-zone flow/pressure | As-built fluid/air circuit with service opening and protected return/discharge path | UNKNOWN |
| PHY-TSC03 | No unexpected hazardous coolant/air release during access | M89, M9, pump OFF, purge timer and HMI green are not physical isolation | Lockable source isolation and independent zone-specific safe-pressure verification; no energized line opening | NOT RUN |
| DEP-TSC04 | Spring/ball vs shuttle/ball replacement, slotted sensor elbow and firmware alarm changes | A plausible analog pressure sample can coexist with wrong flow or a check fault | Approved variant, inspection/change records and qualified revalidation of failure path | UNKNOWN |
| HF-TSC05 | Accessible filters, check valves, guards and tool-change zone | Difficult service encourages guard defeat or live fitting loosening | Accessible lockable isolation, shielded verification, correct stocked parts and maintainable guard | UNKNOWN |
| VAL-TSC06 | Actual machine-specific protection and deliberate restart | Normal LinuxCNC or Haas M-code cannot own personnel safety | Qualified no-person-exposure physical verification plan, independent protective path if required, safe handback | NOT RUN |

**No machine-specific pressure, flow, injection threshold, stop time, PL/SIL, valve truth table or test is claimed.** OSHA 1910.147 (d)(5)/(d)(6) applies to covered servicing and residual energy. Product-specific diagnostic setpoints are not injury thresholds. Conditional TSC-1..6 evidence belongs in the existing restricted Stage A/Stage B process **only if equipment is installed**. OpenPressBrake G0/G1 HOLD, 2520–25F0 external evaluator OPEN, 0 hosted/local compute.

**Exact LinuxCNC M-code collision and M6 source correction (2026-10-27):** LinuxCNC's [stable M-code manual](https://www.linuxcnc.org/docs/stable/html/nb/gcode/m-code.html) explicitly defines **M73 as save-and-autorestore modal state**, while Haas TG0145 uses **M73 to start TAB** and **M74 to stop TAB**. Thus M73 is **not absent** in LinuxCNC; it is a dangerous *same-number, different-meaning* portability trap. The LinuxCNC M6 section additionally states that coolant **continues to flow unless M9 turns it off**; never import a Haas toolchange purge/stop expectation into a LinuxCNC M6 without an implemented, verified sequence. Haas M88/M89 are OEM TSC commands, not standard LinuxCNC coolant commands. These are DOC-CONFIRMED **documentation semantics**, not a runtime test or proof of physical safe state.

## 11JS. 4400-APC-01 — Pallet clamp sensor versus physical seating, option-dependent E-stop (2026-10-27; NON-GRADUATED)
**New OEM-specific delta:** [Haas TG0108/2026 Rev E source audit](../research/4400_APC_01_HAAS_PALLET_CHANGER_EARLY_CLAMP_SENSOR_HYDRAULIC_REVERSE_AND_SERVICE_JUMPER_OEM_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [field card](4400_APC_01_PALLET_CLAMP_SENSOR_ESTOP_OPTION_CHANGE_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-27.md). TG0108 **DOC-CONFIRMS** pallet lowering prematurely when rack/switch geometry trips before proper position, reversed hydraulic lines or sensor wiring, hydraulic oil escaping through an air-blast seal, and context-dependent clamp input states. Haas 2026-10-02 UMC installation distinguishes OEM service jumper with pallet pool **absent** from installed station connection and keyed hydraulic couplings. These are manufacturer-specific diagnostic/installation facts, not validated safety functions.

| ID | Distinct physical or safety proposition | Ordinary misleading indication | Required selected-machine independent witness | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| APC-1 | Selected model, pallet variant, receiver/rack and option state | Generic OEM I/O table or “pallet pool enabled” | As-built machine and correct OEM revision, pallet presence and option state | UNKNOWN |
| APC-2 | Actual mechanical clamp/locator engagement and H-frame endpoint | Clamp, CW/CCW or up/down switch may trip early | Actual pallet seating/retention and qualified mechanical support | UNKNOWN |
| APC-3 | Correct hydraulic clamp/unclamp direction and residual energy | Command/pressure input alone | Keyed routing, physical pressure zones and safe isolation/restraint | UNKNOWN |
| APC-4 | Sensor geometry, polarity and diagnostic context | 0/0 inputs can mean no pallet, not automatically a fault | As-built rack/switch variant and presence-aware interpretation | UNKNOWN |
| APC-5 | Installed load-door/E-stop protective authority | E-stop alarm cleared with OEM absent-option service hardware | Verified option-installed station interface, protective chain and no unreviewed jumper | UNKNOWN |
| APC-6 | Power-loss, partial-index and recovery handback | HMI “ready” after reset | Qualified physical state verification and deliberate restart, no personnel exposure | NOT RUN |
| APC-7 | Easy maintenance, guard reinstallation and anti-defeat | Difficult access invites sensor override/hose swap | Accessible lockable energy control, correct parts, keyed hoses and guards | UNKNOWN |

**No selected machine, hydraulic truth table, sensor timing, stopping distance, PL/SIL, injury, physical test or jumper authorization claimed.** Conditional APC-1..7 only in existing restricted Stage A/Stage B if pallet changer installed; OpenPressBrake applicability UNKNOWN, G0/G1 HOLD, independent 2520–25F0 evaluator OPEN, 0 hosted/local compute.

## 11JT. 4400 comparative lesson — “a valid proxy is not the protected physical state” (2026-10-27)
Two newly inspected, **different** OEM systems expose the same architectural mistake, but with distinct failure mechanisms:
- **TSC/TAB pressure proxy** — [TG0145/AD0086 audit](../research/4400_TSC_TAB_01_HAAS_COOLANT_AIR_CHECK_VALVE_BACKFLOW_PRESSURE_WITNESS_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md): a slotted pressure tap intentionally reports pressure to a sensor while tool flow and check-valve direction are separate physical propositions (**DOC-CONFIRMED topology; INFERENCE if one claims false-positive flow**). Pump OFF and LinuxCNC M9/M6 are not pressure-zero proofs.
- **APC position proxy** — [TG0108/2026 installation audit](../research/4400_APC_01_HAAS_PALLET_CHANGER_EARLY_CLAMP_SENSOR_HYDRAULIC_REVERSE_AND_SERVICE_JUMPER_OEM_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md): manufacturer explicitly reports a rack switch tripping before the pallet is in position, leading to premature lowering (**DOC-CONFIRMED OEM symptom**). Clamp I/O also depends on pallet presence.
- **E-stop diagnostic proxy** — Haas's 2026 **option-absent** service jumper permits an OEM maintenance configuration; an E-stop alarm clearing in that state **does not establish** the protective chain of an **installed** pallet pool. **DOC-CONFIRMED** existence and scope of service hardware; **UNKNOWN** selected machine and safety integrity.

**Practical proof rule:** For every claimed safe state, write (1) the hazardous physical mechanism, (2) the ordinary command, (3) the proxy/sensor and its failure geometry, (4) the independent protective authority, and (5) the physical/qualified validation witness. If (4) or (5) is missing, report **UNKNOWN / HOLD** rather than turning a green I/O bit into safety approval. Do not copy the OEM's I/O table, service jumper, hydraulic truth or pressure threshold to another machine. No new lab, score or release.


## 11JU. 4400-OPB-DRV-05 — ordinary driver fault, event-history and physical safety are different witnesses (2026-10-27)
[Direct MAX22216 Rev. 2 source audit](../research/4400_OPB_MAX22216_FAULT_AUTORECOVERY_CRC_STALE_COMMAND_AND_ENABLE_EVIDENCE_AUDIT_2026-10-27.md) and [practical reviewer](4400_OPB_DRV_05_MAX22216_FAULT_RECOVERY_AND_INDEPENDENT_SAFETY_REVIEW_CARD_2026-10-27.md) refine the existing OpenPressBrake OPB-D04/D08 review; no new owner intake form.

| Distinct witness | False-safe shortcut | Correct evidence allocation | State |
|---|---|---|---|
| Driver fault history | `N_FAULT` not asserted after ENABLE-low means no earlier fault | Separate normal diagnostic log/validity and explicit evidence of event capture; ENABLE-low can clear device registers; no log requirement may delay safety inhibit | SOURCE/DOC only; actual firmware UNKNOWN |
| Actual coil command acceptance | SPI CRC rejected OFF/setpoint means zero current | CRC error leaves prior config; check next-transaction COMER, pipelined readback, and ordinary fresh-command/recovery logic | SOURCE/DOC only; runtime NOT RUN |
| Driver fault effect | Every fault means output tri-stated and latched | Distinguish HHF (no tri-state), OVT (auto-recovery), OCP (channel tri-state/recovery), UVM (register persistence until digital POR) | DOC-CONFIRMED IC only |
| Hardware permit | Both MAX22216 ENABLE LOW means full machine safety | Validate wire-75 conditioning and both ordinary sinks separately; independent Pilz/AKAS, 11K2, hydraulic final elements and actual Y1/Y2 stop/hold/pressure/guard/restart remain required | Machine G0/G1 HOLD |

The ADI datasheet's IC-level enable/disable timing is **not** press-brake stopping time. A normal diagnostic fault or cleared register is never a physical ram/energy witness. This is a documentary source-depth improvement; no board, machine, hydraulic, evaluator or lab validation, no PL/SIL claim and no GitHub-hosted/local compute.


## 11JV. 4400-STD-6909-01 — standards identity is a design input, not a physical safety witness (2026-10-27)
[Original ISO/EN ISO 6909:2026 publisher scope and legacy-machine audit](../research/4400_PRESS_BRAKE_ISO6909_2026_TYPE_C_PUBLICATION_SCOPE_AND_LEGACY_RETROFIT_GATE_2026-10-27.md) corrects the RESCUE-01 edition inventory. **DOC-CONFIRMED:** ISO 6909:2026 published May 29, 2026, specific to hydraulic/servo press brakes; EN adoption and EU 2026/2015 listing exist. Its abstract excludes pre-publication manufactured press brakes and the safety of automatic loading/unloading equipment. ISO 16092-1 explicitly excludes press brakes, so hydraulic-press Part 3 is not an automatic substitute. The exact selected standard edition, date/jurisdiction/retrofit scope, paid normative clauses and physical stop/hold/guard/rearm acceptance are **UNKNOWN**.

|---|---|---|---|
| Machine/edition identity | `hydraulic` implies ISO 16092-3, or 2026 ISO edition applies to all old brakes | Stage A A/F qualified machine technology, manufacturing/retrofit date, jurisdiction, ANSI B11.3/ISO 6909/EN 12622 selection and rationale | UNKNOWN |
| Boundary with automated loading | `press-brake Type-C` covers robot/loading cell safety automatically | Separate integration/occupancy/guard/restart assessment; standard's published exclusion respected | UNKNOWN/N/A only with proof |
| Normative and physical validation | Title/abstract/harmonised citation proves safe Y1/Y2 stop/hold | Licensed applicable clauses + selected safety program + independent physical final-element and stop/hold/energy/guard/restart witnesses | NOT RUN |

**No retroactive certification, legal adoption assumption, numeric safety requirement or machine release.** G0/G1 HOLD, independent evaluator OPEN, 0 hosted/local compute.

## 11JX. 4400-GUARD-FAST-01 — Guard presence, physical retention, safe access and deliberate restart are separate witnesses (2026-10-28)
[EU Annex III / OSHA / captive-fastener manufacturer source-depth](../research/4400_GUARD_FAST_01_EU_ANNEXIII_CAPTIVE_FIXINGS_FALSE_CLOSED_AND_MAINTENANCE_BYPASS_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md) and [one-pass field card](4400_GUARD_FAST_01_CAPTIVE_FASTENER_AND_ACCESS_ANTI_BYPASS_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-28.md) refine existing GUARD-01 without creating another unscored exam. OSHA eTool directly confirms fixed-guard maintenance inconvenience and interlocked access tradeoffs. Clarendon/MW show real captive-hardware solutions, not certified guard performance.

| Guard retention | Panel visually in opening, or a switch says closed | Stage A E: actual fixed fastener/hinge/geometry, securement and selected reach/projectile load path; check false-closed condition | UNKNOWN |

**No EU law assumed for a U.S. machine**, no guard dimensions/impact strength, stopping time, PL/SIL, as-built evidence, test or compute. Do not allow ordinary LinuxCNC/FPGA guard status to substitute for independent safety final elements. Human-factor principle: the correctly installed guard must be easier to maintain than to defeat.


## 11JY. 4400-STRUCT-01 — Press-brake frame/bed/ram load path is independent of tool capacity and Y1/Y2 synchronization (2026-10-28)
[Original Cincinnati EM-519 2019 structural envelope source audit](../research/4400_STRUCT_01_CINCINNATI_PRESS_BRAKE_ECCENTRIC_OFFCENTER_STRIPPING_LOAD_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md) → [practical reviewer](4400_STRUCT_01_THREE_DIRECTION_MACHINE_LOAD_AND_REVERSE_STRIPPING_TECHNICIAN_CARD_2026-10-28.md). Predecessor §11HW TOOL-LOAD-03 already covers local punch/holder ratings. **New `BND/HAZ/PHY`**: machine structural force limit varies with lateral position; a separate front-back eccentric moment acts on bed/ram/bolts; upward stripping reverses force and may have no rating for a bolt-on nose. A correctly synchronized Y1/Y2 and nominal machine tonnage cannot prove these mechanical paths safe.

| **Front-back** eccentric moment within structure | Tool kN/m, Y1/Y2 encoder level, pressure setpoint | Actual offset and force, exact OEM eccentric envelope and nose/part-length condition; qualified structural reviewer | UNKNOWN |
| **UP-stroke stripping** direction/rating | Downstroke force rating | Actual extraction/stripping mode and exact OEM upstroke capacity; cited Cincinnati bolt-on noses explicitly not rated | UNKNOWN |
| Structural fasteners/guards and fresh start | No controller alarm, visually intact panel | Stage A D/E/G: qualified physical bed/nose/attachment inspection, fracture exposure/guard retention, independent stop/hold and deliberate restart | G0/G1 HOLD |

**DOC-CONFIRMED** original OEM pages 4-7/4-8, **INFERENCE** cross-limit review, **UNKNOWN** selected machine. 400 PF OEM example numbers are **not** machine-specific OpenPressBrake values; metric column ambiguity not silently corrected. **No** new owner packet, force calculation, PL/SIL, physical test, external evaluator or compute. G0/G1 HOLD; G2 NOT RUN; G3/G4 NOT AUTHORIZED; G5 HOLD.


## 11JZ. 4400-TANDEM-02 — Visible slave guard and tonnage display do not establish active protection or actual load (2026-10-28)
[Original Cincinnati 2019 exact tandem control and guarding source-depth](../research/4400_TANDEM_02_CINCINNATI_MASTER_ONLY_LIGHT_GUARD_SLAVE_FOOTSWITCH_AND_TONNAGE_DISPLAY_SOURCE_DELTA_2026-10-28.md) refines existing TANDEM-01 (not a new machine claim). **DOC-CONFIRMED OEM:** in its tandem mode, light guard connected to slave **will not be active**; full-span light guard connects to master. Slave foot/palm **cycle commands** inactive, but slave E-stop is listed among active controls. Short bends make programmed/DIAGNOSTIC tonnage differ from actual and can produce off-center/out-of-level loading. **AUTH/DEP:** safety guard mapping is mode-dependent, normal command ownership is distinct from protective-device authority, and ordinary tonnage display is a proxy. **PHY/VAL:** only if selected Stage A proves tandem, obtain actual mode/guard input mapping, both ram final elements, physical stop/hold, workpiece support and qualified load distribution; do not transfer OEM numbers. **HF:** label active guard zone and command ownership at both stations, reject green inactive-sensor indications, demand deliberate restart after mode change. **UNKNOWN:** selected OpenPressBrake tandem existence and all physical proof. No new bank, score, test, PL/SIL or compute; G0/G1 HOLD.


## 11KA. FORM-HAZ-04 original NIOSH fatal workpiece ejection: part-to-die retention is not machine tonnage (2026-10-28)
[Direct original OKFACE/NIOSH FACE 05-OK-005-01 incident audit](../research/4400_FORM_HAZ_04_NIOSH_FACE_05OK005_PRESS_BRAKE_WORKPIECE_EJECTION_INCIDENT_ADDENDUM_2026-10-28.md) establishes an actual reported 2005 fatality: a **920-lb** lug slipped from the **rear lower V-die** during near-edge bending and was ejected into operator/forklift support space. This is **not** the structural bolt overload mechanism of §11JY, nor proof of any OpenPressBrake incident. **BND/HAZ/PHY:** heavy workpiece-to-die engagement and support reaction through every bend increment; possible release/trajectory, operator/helper/forklift exclusion and guard containment. **AUTH:** normal CNC Y1/Y2 position and nominal force, E-stop availability and warning signs do not prevent a released part from striking a person. **HF:** convenient stable fixtures/supports and guarded setup, not reliance on an operator standing in the ejection path. **VAL/CHG:** existing Stage A B/E/F/G and FORM-HAZ-04 unscored cases; actual part geometry, die engagement, support and guards UNKNOWN. No inferred safe distance, force, stop time, PL/SIL, physical test, score or compute; G0/G1 HOLD.


## 11KB. 4400 structural fracture vs tool fracture vs workpiece ejection — evidence discrimination (2026-10-28)
This is a **cross-source reasoning synthesis**, not a new module, scored evaluation or new physical machine fact. The [Cincinnati structural manual](../research/4400_STRUCT_01_CINCINNATI_PRESS_BRAKE_ECCENTRIC_OFFCENTER_STRIPPING_LOAD_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md), [WILA local tool rating predecessor](../research/4400_TOOL_LOAD_03_WILA_LOCAL_TOOL_LOAD_DISTRIBUTION_AND_VERTICAL_HANDLING_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-26.md) and [NIOSH FACE fatal ejection report](../research/4400_FORM_HAZ_04_NIOSH_FACE_05OK005_PRESS_BRAKE_WORKPIECE_EJECTION_INCIDENT_ADDENDUM_2026-10-28.md) support **different propositions**, even though each can injure an operator while a ram executes an apparently normal bend.

| Failure path | Source-grounded trigger | Evidence needed | Wrong shortcut |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Machine structural failure** | OEM warns eccentric load can fracture bed/die-clamp/ram-nose bolts; off-center/reverse capacities separately bounded | Exact machine structure, load center/direction, model-specific OEM envelopes and physical fastener condition | Nominal press tonnage or synchronized Y1/Y2 proves structural safety |
| **Tool/holder failure** | WILA local per-length and variant ratings differ; uniform load assumption matters | Exact tool/holder/adapter, engaged length and actual contact distribution | Global machine capacity proves a short punch safe |
| **Workpiece ejection without reported machine fracture** | NIOSH FACE 05-OK-005: heavy lug slips from rear V-die under near-edge load and strikes operator | Actual workpiece/die engagement, supports, possible ejection trajectory, guarding and personnel positions | Safe frame/tool force and an E-stop prove the workpiece cannot escape |

**SRS/PHY:** capacity compliance, part retention, physical guarding and independent stop/hold are **complementary**, not substitutes. **VAL/CHG:** a tool/part/offset/support change can invalidate multiple separate witnesses. **HF:** present simple task-specific setup limits and usable supports/guards instead of forcing operators to guess from one tonnage display. Selected OpenPressBrake values and validation remain UNKNOWN; G0/G1 HOLD; no physical test, compute or evaluator.

The low-level/thermal trip still does not prove heater deenergization, physical ram holding or personnel-safe access. G0/G1 HOLD.

### 11LF — Operator state labels must not promote ordinary commands into safety authority (2026-10-28; synthesis, NOT a new module)
This crosscut reconciles [M67/M68 analog queue source](../research/4400_ANALOG_QUEUE_01_LINUXCNC_2910_M67_M68_PENDING_REASSERTION_AND_SAFETY_AUTHORITY_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md), [M70/M72/M73 modal restoration](../research/4400_MODAL_RESTORE_01_LINUXCNC_2910_M70_M72_M73_SPINDLE_RESTART_AND_COOLANT_RESTORE_SOURCE_AUDIT_2026-10-28.md), and [ATC M61 identity source addendum](4400_ATC_01_TOOL_RETENTION_DRAWBAR_PNEUMATIC_RELEASE_AND_SERVICE_MODULE_2026-10-25.md). It is **INFERENCE human-factors architecture** grounded in **SOURCE-CONFIRMED ordinary control semantics**, not an independently evaluated interface or physical machine.

| Layer | Display wording that preserves authority | Misleading wording to avoid | Required independent evidence before access |
|---|---|---|---|
| Normal LinuxCNC analog/HAL | “Process analog command: 0.0 (requested)” plus pending/queued state when known | “Hydraulics SAFE / energy zero” | Selected independent stop and actual final-element/ram-hold proof |
| Normal program/modal state | “M5 requested; saved M3 may be restored by M72/M73” | “Spindle locked out” | Independent standstill/guard and service isolation where needed |
| Normal tool identity | “Current tool number: N (operator/program assignment)” | “Tool physically verified and clamped” | Selected tool-ID, retention/capture and guarded-zone evidence |
| Safety-related control | “Safety system request / channel diagnostics / reset pending” with qualified selected device identity | “Safe” based solely on one green bit or reset | Qualified safety-function validation and final-element physical witness |
| Physical isolation / gravity restraint | “Isolation verified by qualified procedure” and explicit dated witness | “Off because PC/FPGA is off” | Actual stored-energy dissipation, mechanical support/holding and authorized restart |

**Implementation contract:** distinguish **REQUESTED**, **OBSERVED**, **SAFETY-PERMITTED**, and **PHYSICALLY VERIFIED** in data models and HMI, with provenance, timestamp, freshness, fault and responsible authority; never silently infer one from another. Use text+symbols rather than color alone. Missing/expired safety witness must not become “safe” by default. Make guards quick to reinstall, provide a clear low-friction safe setup/service path, and separate safety reset from fresh motion start. **No ordinary LinuxCNC/HMI status grants personnel-safety authority**; a qualified independent safety-related controller may implement validated safety logic, while selected physical final elements must establish the required safe state. G0/G1 HOLD; external evaluator OPEN; no machine test or compute.

Stage A only if installed; G0/G1 HOLD.

### §11LX — HOSE-SHIELD-01 abrasion, jet containment, hose whip and gravity hold (2026-10-29; documentary only)
[Original Parker/Gates exact-OEM source delta](../research/4400_HOSE_SHIELD_01_GATES_LIFEGUARD_PARKER_UHP_ABRASION_VS_PRESSURE_CONTAINMENT_SOURCE_DELTA_2026-10-29.md) → [technician card](4400_HOSE_SHIELD_01_ABRASION_WRAP_IS_NOT_SPRAY_CONTAINMENT_FIELD_CARD_2026-10-29.md). **BND/HAZ:** operator/service sightline to a hydraulic hose can expose a person to a high-pressure jet, end-fitting whip, redirected discharge and hidden wear, separately from ram crush. **SRS/DEP:** conditional on installed hoses, record `HS-HOSE` assembly identity/pressure/temperature/medium; `HS-COVER` exact abrasion vs rated pressure-containment type and revision; `HS-END` sleeve end clamps and actual discharge direction; `HS-WHIP` independently rated hose-end grip/anchor where needed; `HS-INSPECT` visible condition and replacement access; `HS-ENERGY` each stored-pressure volume and independent gravity restraint; `HS-CHG` replacement hose, cover, clamp, route and guard revision. **AUTH:** LinuxCNC/FPGA alarms and green statuses have no personnel-safety authority; independent selected safety system and physical final elements must prove stop/hold/isolation separately. **DOC-CONFIRMED:** Parker original UHP guide says abrasion shields do not resist high-pressure leakage; Gates LifeGuard's conditional up-to-five-minute product claim is not an operator exposure time. **UNKNOWN/NOT RUN:** installed OpenPressBrake hose/sleeve/pressure/stop/hold, OEM-approved matched assembly, discharge path and independent witness; no pressure test, PL/SIL or numeric design transfer. G0/G1 HOLD, external evaluator OPEN.


**§11LX same-session electrical-property discriminator:** Gates lists LifeGuard sleeve material as non-conductive; Parker 4400-B.1 §2.1.1 separately requires specific marked/approved nonconductive **hose and fitting assembly** for applications relying on electrical isolation. Do not credit a plastic spray cover as dielectric machine protection. Selected electrical environment and full assembly conductivity UNKNOWN; no energized test.

G0/G1 HOLD; evaluator OPEN; no runtime or machine test.

Do not assume G53 zero equals configured parked/home pose, especially with nontrivial kinematics. Selected config and clearance remain UNKNOWN; no personnel-safety credit.

A top-level warning alone can be missed; local task wording now prevents an unchecked documentary field from silently authorizing cabinet/guard access. **SOURCE-CONFIRMED documentation change only; physical Stage A A–G and Y1/Y2 stop/hold still UNKNOWN, G0/G1 HOLD.**

SOURCE-CONFIRMED public mapping; INFERENCE conditional fault cases; TEST-CONFIRMED none; G0/G1 HOLD.

**§11MD default-limit correction:** v2.9.10 source `inispindle.cc` defaults motion max to `1e99` if `[SPINDLE_n]MAX_FORWARD_VELOCITY` is absent, while pinned INI docs say ~2.147e9 RPM. Numeric discrepancy UNKNOWN; neither is an independent or practical speed cap. With both D and INI max absent, the code's downstream clamp does not establish a safe RPM ceiling. Qualified actual machine evidence remains required.
